### [1] [TACET: Context-Appropriate Acoustic-Social Navigation for Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03828)
*Sungsan Park,Young-Sik Shin,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TACET提出一种情境自适应的声学-社会导航方法，通过视觉语言推理器与反应式控制器结合，同时决定四足机器人"往哪走"和"多大声走"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人进入医院、养老院和安静办公室时，其运动噪声（主要来自足-地碰撞）本身是一种社会变量。既有社会导航只尊重人类空间但视机器人为声学均匀体，而安静运动方法仅按操作者指定降低噪声、忽视情境。因此需要同时考虑空间与声学的社会适配导航。

Method: 提出TACET：从机器人自我中心视角推断社会情境。将慢速微调的视觉语言推理器与快速反应式控制器通过一个紧凑的行为token<gait, speed, social_cost>耦合。该token同时条件化社会代价地图（去哪）与安静运动策略（多大声）。并用结构化视野外记忆，在人物离开相机视野后仍保留在推理器上下文中。

Result: 在真实四足机器人上，情境条件化运动在相同速度下最多降低9.3 dBA噪声；各场景中完整方法保持100%的个人空间合规，声学侵入低（<=2.9 dBA），在所评估场景中同时提升了空间与声学表现。

Conclusion: 通过将社会空间需求与声学安静需求统一到一个行为token中，TACET实现了情境自适应的声学-社会导航，在真实四足平台上兼顾个人空间合规与低噪声侵入。

Abstract: Quadruped robots entering hospitals, care homes, and quiet offices must be context-appropriate not only in where they move but in how loudly they move: a legged robot's locomotion noise, dominated by foot-ground impacts, is itself a social variable. Prior social navigation respects human space but treats the robot as acoustically uniform, while quiet-locomotion methods reduce noise to an operator-specified, context-blind level. We present TACET, a context-appropriate acoustic-social navigation method that infers social context from the robot's egocentric view and decides both where it walks and how loudly, coupling a slow fine-tuned vision-language reasoner to a fast reactive controller through a single compact behavior token, <gait, speed, social_cost>. The same token conditions both a social costmap (where to go) and a quiet locomotion policy (how loudly to move), while a structured out-of-view memory keeps recently seen people in the reasoner's context after they leave the camera view. On a real quadruped, context-conditioned locomotion lowers locomotion noise by up to 9.3 dBA at matched speed, and across our scenarios the full method keeps personal-space compliance at 100% with low acoustic intrusion (<=2.9 dBA), jointly improving spatial and acoustic performance in the evaluated scenarios. The project page is available at https://rcilab.khu.ac.kr/tacet/.

</details>


### [2] [GOTT: Object-centric Dexterous Manipulation with a Reusable Cross-Embodiment Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03861)
*Yulin Liu,Lai Wei,Yen-Jen Wang,Akash Sharma,Pieter Abbeel,Henrik I. Christensen,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GOTT是一个以跨本体接触获取原语为核心的"到达-获取-移动"框架，实现从意图到多指机器人行为的可靠转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型与大规模人类数据提供了丰富的操作意图，但将这些意图转化为多指机器人行为仍然困难；灵巧手缺乏可复用的底层原语，难以跨任务、跨本体可靠地建立接触。

Method: 提出GOTT框架，围绕单一跨本体接触获取原语构建，包含三步：给定机器人无关的物体轨迹与到达规格，先将手靠近任务相关接触区域；共享的闭环原语从近似初始化建立稳定接触；再由姿态条件控制器跟踪期望的物体运动。到达规格可来自未来感知规划、外部模型或人类演示，而原语与跟踪后端保持不变。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，GOTT能在多样物体、臂手平台以及已见和未见的手机形态上建立稳健接触，并相较开环抓取执行持续提升端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: 接触获取原语可作为可复用的灵巧操作底层模块，支持跨任务与跨本体的稳健接触建立，并有效提升整体任务表现。

Abstract: Foundation models and large-scale human data provide rich sources of manipulation intent, but translating this intent into multi-fingered robot behavior remains difficult. Dexterous hands still lack a reusable low-level primitive that reliably establishes contact across tasks and embodiments. We propose GOTT, a reach-acquire-move framework built around a single cross-embodiment contact-acquisition primitive. Given a robot-agnostic object trajectory and a reach specification, GOTT first brings the hand near a task-relevant contact region. The shared closed-loop primitive then establishes stable contact from this approximate initialization, and a pose-conditioned controller tracks the desired object motion. Reach specifications may come from future-aware planning, external models, or human demonstrations, while the primitive and tracking backend remain unchanged. Simulation and real-world experiments show that GOTT is able to establish robust contact across diverse objects, arm-hand platforms, and seen and unseen hand morphologies. It also consistently improves end-to-end task success over open-loop grasp execution.

</details>


### [3] [MOSAIC-SV: Real-Time Adaptive Identification of Vessel Dynamics for the Control and Deployment of Aquatic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03898)
*Wensen Liu,Jerry Peng,Shravani Vedagiri,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MOSAIC-SV，一种可部署的实时自适应水动力辨识与控制系统，无需专门辨识试验，仅凭规格表工程先验即可在闭环任务每个控制步重新估计动力学模型，并在仿真与实船试验中显著优于冻结的先验模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的水面机器人控制依赖水动力模型，而此类模型辨识成本高，且高度依赖特定船体、载荷与测量工况。本文旨在无需专门辨识试验、仅借工程先验的情况下，实现可在线自适应的控制充分动力学辨识。

Method: 提出MOSAIC-SV系统：采用物理可行的无迹卡尔曼滤波，在闭环任务每个控制步重新估计水动力、扰动与执行器参数；引入命令相关的consider投影，抑制当前指令无法归因于执行器效能与外力之间的修正；外层由模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器基于当前估计进行规划。

Result: 在CyberShip II仿真中，MOSAIC-SV在静态失配与瞬态变化下恢复了标定模型的航行性能，且在惯量或阻尼先验错误一个数量级时，航行时间仍在未缩放先验的20%以内。在BlueBoat双推进器双体船实船试验中，相较冻结的工程先验，MOSAIC-SV航行至少快25%，自身运动预测误差至少降低56%，并能在未建模载荷下保持性能。同一系统概念亦在6.3吨双舷外单体船上完成可行部署。

Conclusion: MOSAIC-SV证明了无需专门辨识试验、从工程先验出发即可在线辨识控制充分动力学模型并实时更新控制的可行性，在仿真、小型实船及大型船舶上均具备有效性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Model-based control of an aquatic robotic platform depends on a hydrodynamic model that is costly to identify and specific to the hull, payload, and conditions it was measured in. Here, we present MOSAIC-SV, a deployable real-time adaptive dynamics identification and control system that identifies a control-sufficient dynamics model from a spec-sheet engineering prior, without dedicated identification trials, and re-estimates it at every control step of a closed-loop mission while the controller plans on it. A physically admissible unscented Kalman filter re-estimates hydrodynamic, disturbance, and actuator parameters at every control step of the closed-loop mission; while a command-dependent consider projection withholds corrections the current command cannot attribute between actuator effectiveness and external force; and a model predictive path integral controller plans on the current estimate. In simulation on a CyberShip II plant, MOSAIC-SV recovers the transit performance of the calibrated model under static mismatch and transient changes, and stays within 20% of its own transit time at the unscaled prior when its inertia or damping prior is wrong by an order of magnitude. In on-water field trials on the Blue Robotics BlueBoat, a twin-thruster catamaran, MOSAIC-SV transits at least 25% faster and predicts its own motion with at least 56% less error than its frozen engineering prior, including under an unmodeled payload. The same MOSAIC-SV system concept was also feasibly deployed on a 6.3-tonne dual outboard monohull.

</details>


### [4] [Sparse Calibration-Based Personalization of Kernel-Based Gait Phase and Speed Estimation Using Wearable IMUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03931)
*Myeongju Cha,Pilwon Hur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于稀疏校准的个性化方法，用于可穿戴IMU的步态相位与步速联合估计，并验证其嵌入式实时可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接应用群体平均核（population-average kernels）可能无法适应个体差异，因此需要个性化校准以提升步态相位与步速估计精度。

Method: 构建28种速度的参考库，基于20名受试者的双侧大腿与小腿轨迹；用3个校准速度估计用户特定基线，并结合基线中心化运动学偏差的主成分（PC）模型，生成个性化核而非直接用群体平均核。

Result: 离线验证中，个性化核较群体核降低了相位误差。单参与者在线试点中，个性化核的平均相位与步速误差数值上更低，并在嵌入式硬件上实时运行。但步速效应不显著，多重比较校正后成对相位差异也不显著。

Conclusion: 试点评估证明了可穿戴实现的可行性，同时指出参考库与在线数据集之间存在不匹配，需要更大规模队列验证。

Abstract: This paper presents sparse calibration-based personalization for kernel-based gait phase and walking speed co-estimation from wearable inertial measurement units (IMUs). A 28-speed reference library was constructed from bilateral thigh and shank trajectories in a 20-subject locomotion dataset. Rather than directly applying population-average kernels, the method combines a user-specific baseline estimated from three calibration speeds with a principal-component (PC) model of baseline-centered kinematic deviations. Offline validation showed that personalized kernels reduced phase error relative to the population kernel. In a single-participant online pilot evaluation, the personalized kernels produced numerically lower mean phase and speed errors and ran in real time on embedded hardware. However, the speed effect was not significant, and pairwise phase differences did not remain significant after multiple-comparison correction. The pilot evaluation establishes wearable implementation feasibility while indicating reference-to-online dataset mismatch and the need for larger-cohort validation.

</details>


### [5] [REDIRECT: A 1% Fix for Bad Robot Habits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03997)
*Yu Zhang,Jiazhuo Li,Yancong Wei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REDIRECT 是一种低成本方法，仅用完整训练样本-反向传播预算的 1%，通过定位并修复混合质量机器人数据中的局部问题行为，大幅恢复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人示教数据中正常与问题演示大部分行为相同，仅在局部动作延续上有差异；完整重训成本高，而仅用干净数据微调在小预算下恢复有限。

Method: 仅利用回合级“保留/问题”标签，REDIRECT 定位问题分支，将一致的保留延续动作分配给该处原始观测，并对共享行为进行锚定，无需帧级标注或额外交互。

Result: 在三个随机化 ManiSkill 任务、三个随机种子下，平均成功率从 68.2% 提升至 90.3%，恢复干净重训差距的 88.8%（匹配算力微调仅 22.1%）；在 PiPER 机械臂的倒杯与叠毛巾任务中恢复干净训练差距的 86.7–92.9%。

Conclusion: 本地机器人不良习惯可通过将更新预算用于修复行为差异而非重新学习共享行为来修复。

Abstract: Robots can acquire bad habits from a few defective moments in otherwise useful teleoperation. In mixed-quality robot data, normal and problematic demonstrations share most task behavior and differ only at a local action continuation. Full retraining is costly, while fine-tuning on clean data alone offers limited recovery under a small update budget. We ask whether the local difference can instead be fixed with only 1% of the full-training sample-backward budget. Using only episode-level retained/problematic labels, REDIRECT localizes the branch, assigns a coherent retained continuation to the original observations around it, and anchors shared behavior, without frame-level annotations or additional interaction. Across three randomized ManiSkill tasks and three seeds, REDIRECT raises the mean success rate from 68.2% to 90.3%, recovering 88.8% of the clean-retraining gap versus 22.1% for matched-compute fine-tuning. On a PiPER arm, it recovers 86.7-92.9% of the clean-only gap across cup insertion and towel folding. Local robot habits can therefore be repaired by spending the update budget on the behavioral difference rather than relearning shared behavior.

</details>


### [6] [SUAVE: Unified Video-Action Models via Masked Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04009)
*Rhythm Syed,Jean Mercat,Sedrick Keh,Kushal Arora,Paarth Shah,Aykut Onol,Mengchao Zhang,Tony Dear*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SUAVE是一个单一词表的统一动作-视频模型，用掩码扩散Transformer将语言、视频、动作统一为离散token，通过选择掩码token即可在同一个网络内切换世界模型、机器人策略或视频-动作模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型擅长语义 grounding 与动作预测，但不会在行动前"想象"未来；基于视频扩散的世界动作模型（WAM）能想象未来，却把语言作为冻结条件置于连续潜空间；统一模型则要么自回归逐token解码，要么保留连续视频加辅助动作头，难以兼顾统一性与效率。

Method: 提出SUAVE（Single vocabulary Unified Action-Video modEl）：以掩码扩散Transformer为核心，在共享序列中将语言、视频、动作全部表示为离散token，并以语言为条件生成视频与动作。推理时通过选择掩码哪些token，同一网络可充当世界模型、机器人策略或视频-动作模型。无动作协同训练时，将无标签视频的动作位置填为掩码token并从损失中排除。

Result: 单一SUAVE模型可预测长时程视频并作为策略，在静态与动态操作任务上与专用世界模型和专用动作策略相当。真机上，模型在RTX 5090上1030 ms内生成子目标图像及覆盖1秒运动的动作块，维持2.5次/秒的闭环控制。在机器人视频上预训练并在人类视频上协同训练，显著提升策略性能与对分布偏移的零样本鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果表明掩码扩散是统一视频-动作建模的一种实用且通用的基础范式，兼顾想象未来与动作生成，并受益于大规模无动作视频协同训练。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) inherit strong semantic grounding from pretrained vision-language backbones but are typically optimized for predicting actions rather than future observations. They can see and act, but they do not imagine the future before acting. World action models (WAMs) built on video diffusion backbones can imagine but treat language as frozen conditioning on a continuous latent space. Unified models bring these modalities into one architecture, but they either decode autoregressively, one token at a time, or keep video continuous with an auxiliary action head. In this work, we present SUAVE, a Single vocabulary Unified Action-Video modEl in which a masked diffusion transformer generates video and actions conditioned on language, with all three modalities represented as discrete tokens in a shared sequence. Choosing which tokens to mask at inference turns the same network into a world model, a robot policy, or a video-action model. For action-free co-training, the action positions of unlabeled video are filled with mask tokens and excluded from the loss. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate two findings. First, a single SUAVE model predicts long-horizon video and acts as a policy, competitive with dedicated world models and specialized action policies on static and dynamic manipulation tasks. On a real robot, our model generates subgoal images and an action chunk spanning one second of motion in 1,030 ms on an RTX 5090 GPU, sustaining closed-loop control at 2.5 actions per second. Second, pretraining on robot video and co-training on human video substantially improves policy performance and zero-shot robustness to distribution shift. Together, these results show that masked diffusion is a practical and versatile foundation for unified video-action modeling.

</details>


### [7] [Return-to-Home Feasible Micro-Aerial Vehicle Exploration for 3D Gaussian Splatting Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04013)
*Prajit Krisshnakumar,Fan Yang,Koichiro Niinuma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出拓扑优先的主动重建框架，将高保真渲染与导航解耦：以3DGS为重建目标，用TSDF/occupancy构建稀疏Voronoi骨架路线图，并通过验证与飞行时间预算感知的视点选择提升室内MAV重建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAV室内快速3D重建需在严格飞行时间预算与安全约束下运行，须显式保证返航（RTH）可行并保留保守裕度，因此需要兼顾重建质量与安全导航的框架。

Method: 1) 解耦高保真渲染与导航：密集3DGS地图作为重建目标；2) 轻量TSDF/occupancy脚手架用于保守碰撞检测与在线构建稀疏3D Voronoi骨架路线图；3) 针对TSDF在线融合噪声与不完整导致的路线图不稳定，用已雕刻自由空间一致性与密集可见性检查验证节点与边，抑制墙后幻影结构；4) 在路线图上以飞行时间预算感知目标并结合轻量滚动时域前瞻选择视点，改善非短视探索。

Result: 在ReplicaCAD、Gibson、HM3D等逼真室内仿真基准上评估，报告重建覆盖率/误差、RTH成功率与计算开销；在相同飞行时间预算下，相对近期基于GS的基线取得更优或具竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 该拓扑优先的主动重建框架在飞行时间受限条件下，通过解耦导航与渲染并稳定路线图规划，实现了兼顾重建质量、返航安全与计算成本的室内MAV 3D重建。

Abstract: Micro aerial vehicles (MAVs) enable rapid indoor 3D reconstruction for inspection and time-critical situational awareness, but must operate under strict flight-time budgets and safety constraints that require explicit return-to-home (RTH) feasibility with a conservative margin. We present a topology-first active reconstruction framework that decouples high-fidelity rendering from navigation. A dense 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) map is optimized as the reconstruction target, while a lightweight Truncated Signed Distance Field (TSDF)/occupancy scaffold supports conservative collision checking and online construction of a sparse 3D Voronoi skeleton roadmap. Since directly extracting roadmaps from TSDF geometry can be unstable under noisy and incomplete online fusion, we validate nodes and edges using carved free-space consistency and dense visibility checks, which suppress behind-wall phantom structure and stabilize planning. Viewpoints are selected on the roadmap using a flight-time-budget-aware objective and a lightweight receding-horizon lookahead to improve non-myopic exploration behavior. We evaluate on photorealistic indoor simulation benchmarks (ReplicaCAD, Gibson, and HM3D), reporting reconstruction coverage/error, RTH success, and computational cost. Results demonstrate improved or competitive performance relative to recent GS-based baselines under identical flight-time budgets.

</details>


### [8] [A Biomimetic Gaze Control to Restore Head-Neck Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04022)
*Colin Rubow,Haohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于前庭眼反射（VOR）的眼动追踪控制器，用于控制颈部动力外骨骼，以恢复颈部肌无力患者的头颈运动；并设计了可调参数的个人化工具，验证表明其头动更自然、运动意图检测准确率平均提升24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颈部肌无力患者需要动力颈部外骨骼来恢复头颈运动，但现有手持输入设备的控制方式不直观，且对该人群不可行。眼睛与头部在日常生活中协同完成许多任务，因此眼动可作为无需手的控制信号。然而，此前的控制模型缺乏生物行为学依据，也缺少个性化手段，导致头颈运动不自然。

Method: 提出一种新颖的眼动追踪控制器设计，利用前庭眼反射（VOR）来模拟自然的眼-头协调；同时设计个人化工具，包含滑块，允许用户调整控制器参数。本文给出了该系统的技术设计，并在真实用户中进行了验证。

Result: 与之前的眼动追踪模型相比，新控制器通过更自然的速度曲线提供个性化的自然头部运动，并使准确运动意图检测平均提升24%。

Conclusion: 该系统为在个性化与偏好背景下研究眼动追踪控制器提供了有力平台，并最终有望恢复患者群体丧失的头颈活动能力。

Abstract: Powered neck exoskeletons are developed to restore head-neck motions for those with neck muscle weakness. Current methods using hand-held input devices to control these robotic systems are unintuitive or non-viable for these populations. Alternatively, the eyes coordinate with the head to accomplish many daily tasks. Therefore, movements of the eyes can be a useful signal to control the neck exoskeletons without needing to use the hands. However, previous control models lack biobehavioral basis as well as means to personalize the control behavior, causing unnatural head-neck movements. This paper presents a novel eye-tracking controller design leveraging the vestibulo-ocular reflex to emulate natural eyehead coordination. Personalization tools were also designed, consisting of sliders to allow users to adjust the parameters of this controller. Here, we present the technical design and validation of this system in human users. Compared to the previous eye-tracking model, the new controller provides natural head motions personalized to the user through more natural velocity profiles and a 24 percent average increase of accurate motion intent detection. This system provides a powerful platform for studying eye-tracking controllers in the context of personalization and preference, and ultimately restoring the lost head-neck mobility in patient populations.

</details>


### [9] [Reward-DAgger: Robot-Gated Interactive Imitation Learning with General-Purpose Progress-Based Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04054)
*Ryan Li,Yigit Korkmaz,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Reward-DAgger，一种基于通用奖励模型密集进度信号的机器人门控交互式模仿学习框架，用于在部署中检测失败并触发人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在未见环境中性能下降，需要检测失败并教授恢复行为；但现有运行时监控方法依赖任务与策略特定的训练或超参调优，难以跨任务部署，且在策略迭代更新时带来额外开销。

Method: 利用通用奖励模型的密集进度信号决定何时需要人工干预，构建机器人门控的交互式模仿学习框架；该方法与底层策略架构无关，无需访问策略内部信息，且门控机制无需针对不同任务重新调参。

Result: 在失败检测的准确率-延迟权衡上优于现有运行时监控基线；在八个仿真与真实任务中持续提升下游策略的自主成功率，并取得较高的人类投入回报，在多数设置下超越基线。

Conclusion: 同一门控配置即可跨任务、环境和策略架构迁移，无需任务特定超参调优，验证了方法的通用性与有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot learning have enabled generalist control policies capable of completing a wide range of tasks. However, their performance degrades when deployed in unseen environments, making it critical to detect failures and teach recovery behaviors. Existing runtime monitoring methods often require task- and policy-specific training or hyperparameter tuning, limiting cross-task deployment and introducing additional overhead during iterative policy updates. We present Reward-DAgger, a robot-gated interactive imitation learning framework that uses dense progress signals from a general-purpose reward model to determine when human intervention is needed. Our approach is agnostic to the underlying policy architecture, requires no access to policy internals, and can be applied across tasks without retuning the gating mechanism. Our results show that Reward-DAgger achieves a better failure-detection accuracy-latency tradeoff than existing runtime monitoring baselines. Across eight simulated and real-world tasks, Reward-DAgger consistently improves the downstream policy's autonomous success rate throughout interactive learning and achieves strong return on human effort, outperforming the baselines in most settings. Importantly, the same gating configuration is used across tasks without task-specific hyperparameter tuning, demonstrating transfer across tasks, environments, and policy architectures. Code and videos are available at https://liralab.usc.edu/reward-dagger.

</details>


### [10] [Terrain-Dependent Intra-Cycle Leg Timing for Effective Locomotion on Granular Slopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04144)
*Long Zhao,Xingjue Liao,Feifei Qian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究以六足机器人在可倾斜颗粒床上的实验为基础，测量坡度相关的法向与剪切阻力，建立机器人-地形相互作用模型，预测锚定时机、步长与速度，并构建失效相图以评估颗粒斜坡上的运动风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在沙丘等颗粒斜坡上，颗粒介质剪切强度降低且受重力诱导出现各向异性屈服，使足式机器人运动极为困难。作者希望揭示坡度导致性能下降的物理机制，并为在可变形斜坡上的安全稳定运动提供预测性指导。

Method: 采用六足机器人在可倾斜颗粒床上系统测量运动速度，以及随坡度变化的法向和剪切颗粒阻力；据此建立一个简化的机器人-地形相互作用模型，用于预测锚定时机、步长和机器人速度，并将模型推广到一般地形条件以构建失效相图。

Result: 法向穿透阻力随倾角几乎不变，而剪切阻力随坡度增大显著降低。模型表明，坡度导致的性能损失主要源于锚定延迟和后向滑移增加，而非过度下陷。推广模型后得到失效相图，可区分下陷型与滑移型失效区域，实现定量风险估计。

Conclusion: 该物理信息框架可预测颗粒斜坡上地形相关的失效机制，为足式机器人在可变形斜坡上的更安全、更稳健运行提供指导。

Abstract: Locomotion on granular slopes such as sand dunes remains a fundamental challenge for legged robots due to reduced shear strength and gravity-induced anisotropic yielding of granular media. Using a hexapedal robot on a tiltable granular bed, we systematically measure locomotion speed together with slope-dependent normal and shear granular resistive forces. While normal penetration resistance remains nearly unchanged with inclination, shear resistance decreases substantially as slope angle increases. Guided by these measurements, we develop a simple robot-terrain interaction model that predicts anchoring timing, step length, and resulting robot speed, as functions of terrain strength and slope angle. The model reveals that slope-induced performance loss is primarily governed by delayed anchoring and increased backward slip rather than excessive sinkage. By extending the model to generalized terrain conditions, we construct failure phase diagrams that identify sinkage- and slippage-induced failure regimes, enabling quantitative risk estimation for locomotion on granular slopes. This physics-informed framework provides predictive insight into terrain-dependent failure mechanisms and offers guidance for safer and more robust robot operation on deformable inclines.

</details>


### [11] [Autoware in Construction: Gap Analysis and LiDAR Perception Toward Off-Road Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04187)
*Yu Otsuki,Teja Emmey,Sena Matsushita,Akiro Harada,Takeshi Miura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文分享将 Autoware 自动驾驶系统扩展到建筑工地大型自卸卡车过程中的经验教训，分析公路与越野场景在各模块的差异并提出解决方案与路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Autoware 最初面向公路场景（乘用车、出租车、公交），如今正被拓展到建筑、农业等越野环境。但建筑工地的非结构化地形、扬尘、持续变化的场地条件及专用物体，从根本上挑战了传统自动驾驶系统的诸多假设，因此需要总结其中的差距与应对经验。

Method: 以 Autoware 为基础，面向建筑工地大型自卸卡车，系统梳理公开道路与越野建筑应用在感知、建图、定位、环境感知、规划与控制等模块上的差距；讨论相应解决方案，重点介绍基于 LiDAR 的感知流水线开发现状与现场测试发现。

Result: 识别出公路与建筑工地越野应用在 Autoware 各核心模块（传感、建图、定位、感知、规划、控制）上的具体差距，并给出针对性的潜在解决方案，尤其是 LiDAR 感知流水线的开发进展与实地测试结论。

Conclusion: 建筑工地场景与传统公路存在根本差异，需针对性改造 Autoware 各模块；论文最后提出面向建筑车辆端到端自动驾驶的发展路线图。

Abstract: Autoware is an open-source autonomous driving software platform widely adopted by researchers and industry developers. Originally developed primarily for public-road applications, including passenger vehicles, taxis, and buses, Autoware is increasingly being extended to off-road environments such as construction and agricultural sites. Construction sites, however, differ fundamentally from public roads and challenge many assumptions underlying conventional autonomous driving systems. They are characterized by unstructured terrain, airborne dust, continuously evolving site conditions, and construction-specific objects. This paper presents lessons learned from ongoing efforts to extend an Autoware-based autonomous driving system to large dump trucks operating at construction sites. We identify gaps between public-road and off-road construction applications across the sensing, mapping, localization, perception, planning, and control modules of the Autoware stack. We then discuss potential solutions for addressing these gaps, with particular emphasis on ongoing LiDAR-based perception pipeline development and findings from field testing. Finally, we propose a roadmap toward end-to-end autonomous driving for construction vehicles.

</details>


### [12] [Bi-manual Stabilization of the Cervical Spine for Safe Physical Human-Robot Interaction in Rolling Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04220)
*Elizabeth Peiros,Moira Bohley,Lucas Yager,Derek Chen,Rebecca Fellbaum,Chris D'Ambrosia,Ananya Rao,Xiao Liang,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双臂机器人颈部稳定算法，用于在翻滚搬运中保护颈椎，通过优化抓握力与控制颈部旋转，实现接近人类水平的轴向对齐精度（与人类新手相差0.21度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颈椎脆弱且活动度高，在战场、灾害或运动等场景中搬运伤员时极易造成灾难性损伤，因此需要格外小心地稳定颈椎。目前缺乏能够自动完成颈部稳定操作的机器人方法，故研究双臂机器人执行该任务的技术。

Method: 提出一种用于双臂机器人在翻滚动作中稳定颈椎的算法及其适配方法；研究在安全约束内最大化抓握力的技术；并提出一种实现正确颈部旋转的控制方法（含预测机制）。

Result: 双臂机器人能够达到人类水平的旋转对齐保持性能；带预测的机器人轴向对齐误差与人类新手相差在0.21度以内。

Conclusion: 所提方法使双臂机器人可在颈椎稳定任务中实现与人相当的表现，表明机器人有望辅助或承担高风险的颈椎保护性搬运操作。

Abstract: The neck, specifically the cervical spine, is a highly fragile and mobile part of the body with a high risk of catastrophic injury. Whether someone is injured on a battlefield, in a natural disaster, or in an athletic event, great care is always taken with the cervical spine when transporting, rolling, or moving the patient. In this work, we present an algorithm and adaptations for bi-manual robots to stabilize the cervical spine during rolling maneuvers. We investigate techniques to maximize grip strength within safety bounds and present a control approach for proper neck rotation. We found that with our approach, a bi-manual robot is capable of achieving human-level performance in maintaining target rotational alignments. The axial alignment for the robot with prediction was within .21 degrees of human novices.

</details>


### [13] [Continual Humanoid Motion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04231)
*Zhewen He,Hao Huang,Geeta Chandra Raju Bethala,Chong Yu,Tao Chen,Anthony Tzes,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Similarity-guided LoRA-PNN，一种基于渐进神经网络和低秩适配的人形全身运动持续学习控制器，通过运动相似度度量决定知识复用与容量分配，避免灾难性遗忘并实现高效前向迁移，最终部署于Unitree G1实体机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形全身控制器多为离线训练后冻结，学习新技能会侵蚀已掌握的技能，无法在序列任务流中持续获取技能而不遗忘旧知识，因此需要研究人形全身运动的持续学习问题。

Method: 提出Similarity-guided LoRA-PNN，一种渐进神经网络（PNN）策略，结构上防止灾难性遗忘，并通过轻量级低秩适配（LoRA）跨技能复用知识。采用两级运动相似度度量（基于动态时间规整并经最优传输聚合）决定基于哪个旧技能构建以及分配多少新容量。

Result: 在六个顺序学习的技能类别上，该方法取得最佳前向迁移（0.125 对比 0.079）和最高的平均准确率，同时节省最多94.5%的可训练参数和40.8%的训练时间。所得控制器达到96.13%的sim-to-sim迁移率，并部署在实体Unitree G1上。

Conclusion: 相似度引导的LoRA渐进网络能有效实现人形全身运动的持续学习，在防止遗忘、促进前向迁移、提升参数与时间效率方面表现优异，并成功迁移至实体机器人，验证了方法的实用性。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body controllers can now track a diverse set of dynamic motions, but they are typically trained offline and then frozen, so teaching such a controller a new skill tends to erode the skills it already mastered. We study continual learning for humanoid whole-body motion, where a single controller must acquire skills from a sequential task stream without revisiting past data. We introduce Similarity-guided LoRA-PNN, a progressive neural network (PNN) policy that prevents catastrophic forgetting by construction while reusing knowledge across skills through lightweight low-rank adaptation. A two-level motion-similarity measure, built from dynamic time warping aggregated by optimal transport, decides which prior skill to build on and how much new capacity to allocate, yielding strong forward transfer and large efficiency gains. Across six sequentially learned skill categories, our similarity-guided LoRA policy attains the best forward transfer (0.125 vs. 0.079) and the highest average accuracy among all methods, while saving up to 94.5% of trainable parameters and 40.8% of training time. The resulting controller reaches 96.13% sim-to-sim transfer and is deployed on a physical Unitree G1. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/continual-humanoid-learning-35D3.

</details>


### [14] [Humanoid Rickshaw Pulling: Whole-Body Locomotion under Coupled Wheeled Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04238)
*Yangzhi Yang,Xiansheng Lin,Zhaoming Xie,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出人形机器人拉人力车全身控制框架，通过教师-学生蒸馏与强化学习微调，实现重载下车辆轨迹跟踪与平衡维持，硬件上无需重调即可拉动含乘客、总质量达115kg的人力车。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人通过拉被动轮式车辆可运输远超自重的载荷，但由此产生耦合运动难题：机器人须与车辆保持持续的上肢接触，同时适应来自载荷、车辆与地形、未知且随构型变化的力，需要在跟踪指令车辆运动的同时保持平衡与稳定抓握。

Method: 提出面向人形机器人拉人力车的全身控制框架。训练阶段由特权教师利用车辆状态、交互力与载荷属性；将其动作与隐变量蒸馏到以历史为条件的学生策略，令其从本体感受响应中隐式推断耦合动力学，随后进行强化学习微调。与No History和Only History基线对比。

Result: 策略实现精确的车辆轨迹跟踪，同时降低车辆振荡、躯干倾斜与驱动代价。Unitree G1可推动人力车并生成与步态同步的全身反应以稳定侧向与横滚运动；拉动重分配关节用力，在多数载荷-速度条件下机器人归一化运输成本代理低于空载行走。硬件上单一策略完成起步、持续拉动、转弯与停止，适应刚性与乘客载荷，载车总质量达115kg且无需按载荷重调。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过协调且持续的人形-车辆交互，可实现鲁棒的重载运输。

Abstract: Humanoid robots could transport payloads substantially heavier than themselves by pulling passive wheeled vehicles instead of carrying the load. This capability, however, creates a coupled locomotion problem: the robot must maintain persistent upper-body contact while adapting to unknown, configuration-dependent forces arising from the payload, vehicle, and terrain. We present a whole-body control framework for humanoid rickshaw pulling that tracks commanded vehicle motion while preserving balance and stable grasps under uncertain load dynamics. During training, a privileged teacher exploits vehicle states, interaction forces, and load properties. Its actions and latent are distilled into a history-conditioned student that implicitly infers coupled dynamics from proprioceptive responses, followed by reinforcement-learning fine-tuning. Comparisons with \emph{No History} and \emph{Only History} baselines show that the resulting policy achieves accurate vehicle tracking while reducing vehicle oscillation, torso tilt, and actuation cost. Behavioral analysis shows that Unitree G1 propels the rickshaw and generates gait-synchronized whole-body reactions that stabilize its lateral and roll motions. Moreover, pulling redistributes joint effort and yields a lower robot-normalized cost-of-transport proxy than unloaded walking over most tested load--speed conditions. On hardware, a single policy performs starting, sustained pulling, turning, and stopping with both rigid payloads and human passengers, handling a loaded rickshaw mass of up to 115~kg without load-specific retuning. These results demonstrate robust heavy-load transportation through coordinated and persistent humanoid--vehicle interaction.

</details>


### [15] [Attention-Based Surface Representation Learning for Robot State Prediction and Open-Ended Surface Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04240)
*Oleg Kushnarev,Alexander Belyaev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展的户外地形分类方法，通过预测机器人下一时刻状态并引入注意力模块，将新地表类别表示为可训练向量，在Belyaev-Kushnarev数据集上达到98.56%、BorealTC上达到94.8%的分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外地面机器人需要理解地形属性以保证可靠运行。已有感知研究多将地形识别建模为训练时类别数固定的分类问题，无法灵活扩展新地表类别，也难以为更高层任务提供支持。

Method: 提出使新地表类别可作为可训练向量加入的方案，便于后续支持更高层任务；采用基于预测机器人下一时刻状态的学习范式，并结合注意力模块进行分类。

Result: 在Belyaev-Kushnarev数据集上分类精度达98.56%，在BorealTC数据集上达94.8%。

Conclusion: 该方法突破了固定类别数的限制，实现可扩展的地表类别表示，并在多个数据集上取得较高的地形分类精度，为户外机器人地形感知提供了有效途径。

Abstract: For ground robots operating in outdoor environments, understanding the properties of the underlying terrain is essential for ensuring reliable operation. In most perception-based studies, this problem is formulated as categorical classification with a fixed number of classes defined during training. We propose an approach that enables new surface classes to be added as trainable vectors, which can subsequently be used to address higher-level tasks. By employing a learning paradigm based on predicting the robot's next state in time and using attention blocks, we improved classification accuracy to 98.56% on the Belyaev-Kushnarev dataset and 94.8% on BorealTC.

</details>


### [16] [ROOT: Discovering Rewards for User-Specified Embodied Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04250)
*Eren Sadikoglu,Aditya Taparia,Xinyuan Liu,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ROOT框架，通过构建持久化实验树并结合视频-语言模型的行为洞察，以观察引导的搜索方式发现与用户意图一致的奖励函数，从而改进具身控制中的奖励设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习用于具身控制受限于奖励设定的困难。现有基于大语言模型的方法虽能从自然语言描述合成奖励函数，但难以捕捉人类真正在意的细微行为属性（如自然步态、姿态、动作风格），因为许多期望行为在视觉上易于识别却难以用奖励函数编码。

Method: 提出ROOT（Reward Optimization via Observable Trees）框架。它不单纯依赖标量训练统计，而是将奖励设计视为观察引导的搜索过程，在持续扩展的实验树上进行。该树存储奖励程序、训练得到的策略、rollout观察结果，以及由视频-语言模型提炼的行为洞察，用于诊断行为失败并指导后续奖励改进。

Result: 在四种具身形态、七个任务上评估ROOT：仿真环境中的Hopper、HalfCheetah、Ant、Unitree Go2，以及真实世界的Unitree Go2。ROOT生成的策略更符合用户意图，优于现有基于LLM的奖励生成方法，实现高达86.8%的运动完整性准确率，并将Vid-LLM行为对齐分数从3.56/5提升至4.14/5（较基线提升16.5%）。人工评估也支持该结果，ROOT在51-63%的成对比较中被偏好。

Conclusion: ROOT通过观察引导的奖励函数搜索，有效提升了具身控制中策略与用户意图的对齐程度，在仿真与真实场景、多种具身形态上均优于现有LLM奖励生成基线，并获得人工评估支持。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for embodied control remains constrained by the difficulty of reward specification. Although recent large language model (LLM)-based methods can synthesize reward functions from natural-language descriptions, they often fail to capture subtle behavioral properties that humans care about, such as natural gait, posture, and movement style. This limitation arises because many desired behaviors are easier to recognize visually than to encode in a reward function. We introduce Reward Optimization via Observable Trees (ROOT), a framework for discovering reward functions that align learned policies with user-specified embodied behaviors. Rather than relying solely on scalar training statistics, ROOT casts reward design as an observation-guided search over a persistent experiment tree that stores reward programs, trained policies, and rollout observations, together with behavioral insights distilled by a video-language model, to diagnose behavioral failures and guide subsequent reward refinements. We evaluate ROOT on seven tasks across four embodiments: simulated Hopper, HalfCheetah, Ant, Unitree Go2, and as well as the real-world Unitree Go2. ROOT produces behaviors that better align with user intent than those generated by existing LLM-based reward-generation methods, achieving up to 86.8% locomotion-completeness accuracy and improving Vid-LLM behavioral alignment from 3.56/5 to 4.14/5, a 16.5% improvement over baselines. Human evaluations further support these results, with ROOT preferred in 51-63% of pairwise comparisons.

</details>


### [17] [Grounded in Time: A Multi-Source Dataset and Benchmark for Temporal Grounding in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04255)
*Yi Wang,Yang Yang,Guangqi Xu,Sumin Lin,Ning Kang,Pengxiang Lu,Xiaotong Chen,Zeyu Xue,Ping Deng,Xing Liu,Chenguang Yang,Zhenyu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 GiT 数据集与基准，用于评估操作任务中对历史事件的语义推理能力，涵盖实验室、家庭与工业场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中，当仅凭当前观测不足以决定动作时，需要从过去的交互中推断任务相关状态。现有记忆增强的 VLA 模型基准测试虽已有进展，但面向应用、依赖历史的语义推理任务仍被低估。

Method: 构建 GiT 数据集与基准，覆盖 18 个双臂任务，包含真实机器人与 UMI 风格的示范，以及仿真数据和基于 ManiSkill 的评估套件（9 个任务）。提供细粒度子任务标注与反事实任务对。评估代表性端到端 VLA 模型。

Result: 仿真与部分真实任务评估显示，代表性端到端 VLA 模型在依赖历史的操作任务上仍有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: GiT 为历史依赖的操作决策提供了可评估的数据与基准，揭示了当前模型在时序语义推理方面的不足。数据集与基准已在项目主页公开。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires inferring task-relevant states from past interactions when the current observation alone is insufficient to determine the appropriate action. Despite progress in benchmarking memory-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) models, application-oriented tasks requiring history-dependent semantic inference remain underrepresented. We introduce GiT (Grounded in Time), a dataset and benchmark for grounding manipulation decisions in past events across biolaboratory, household, and industrial scenarios. It includes real-robot and Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) style demonstrations covering 18 bimanual tasks, together with simulation data and a ManiSkill-based evaluation suite covering nine tasks. Fine-grained subtask annotations and annotated counterfactual task pairs, in which similar current observations require different actions depending on prior events, support policy learning and targeted evaluation of history use. Evaluations of representative end-to-end VLA models in simulation and on selected real-world tasks reveal substantial room for improvement in history-dependent manipulation. The dataset and benchmark are available at the project page.

</details>


### [18] [Real-Time Conformal-Seeded Hybrid Inverse Kinematics for Offset Redundant Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04266)
*Duc Cuong Vu,Van Tung Nguyen,Duc Hai Nguyen,Manh Cuong Nguyen,Vu Trung Tran,Minh Nhat Vu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保形播种的混合逆运动学策略，用于偏移、冗余的7自由度人形机械臂，结合近似解析解生成候选、学习预测器+分裂保形预测排序、以及LM数值精化，实现实时高精度求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏移结构的7自由度冗余机械臂无法获得精确闭式解，实用解析逆运动学只能依赖近似模型；而数值逆运动学虽可在完整模型上高精度求解，却对初始化高度敏感、收敛性不稳。因此需要兼顾速度、精度与鲁棒性的混合方法。

Method: 提出两阶段混合框架并采用保形校准播种：第一阶段用近似解析模型高效枚举有限个候选关节解；第二阶段用轻量学习预测器评估每个候选的精化难度，并通过分裂保形预测将其包装为校准后的难度上界作为选择评分；随后选取排名最优的种子，在完整运动学模型上用Levenberg-Marquardt求解器进行精化。

Result: 在可达目标评估中实现小于40微秒的实时性能与100%成功率；通过覆盖工作空间的大规模随机仿真以及人形机械臂运动规划实验验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法将快速候选生成、带校准难度界的学习式种子排序与精确数值精化相结合，有效克服了偏移冗余臂逆运动学中解析近似精度不足与数值方法初始化敏感的局限。

Abstract: This paper presents a conformal-seeded hybrid strategy for solving inverse kinematics of offset, redundant 7-DoF robot arms of the humanoid class. Analytical inverse kinematics (AIK) provides closed-form solutions with very low computational cost. However, for offset kinematic structures, the exact closed-form solution is generally unavailable, and practical AIK must rely on an approximate or simplified kinematic model. In contrast, numerical inverse kinematics (NIK) can achieve high-precision solutions on the full kinematic model. However, its convergence is highly sensitive to initialization. To overcome these limitations, we propose a two-stage hybrid inverse kinematics framework with conformal-calibrated seed selection. First, an approximate analytical model efficiently enumerates a finite set of candidate joint solutions. Second, we rank these candidates using a lightweight learned predictor of post-refinement difficulty, wrapped by split-conformal prediction into a calibrated upper bound that serves as the selection score. The best-ranked seed is then refined using a Levenberg-Marquardt solver on the full kinematic model. The proposed method combines fast candidate generation, learned seed ranking with a calibrated difficulty bound, and accurate numerical refinement, achieving real-time performance of less than 40us and a success rate of 100% in our evaluation on reachable targets. We validate the approach through large-scale stochastic simulation across the workspace and experimental demonstrations with motion planning on a humanoid robot arm. Demonstration videos are available at https://youtu.be/aeiBmw1XRbw.

</details>


### [19] [Asynchronous Tracking, Optical Communication and 3D Motion Capture using Event-based Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04342)
*Ziwei Wang,Angus Apps,Holly Battisson,Iain Guilliard,Timothy Molloy,Robert Mahony*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ATOC系统，将LED智能信标调制、事件相机检测、光学跟踪与事件数据解调集成于单一流程，实现多信标同时跟踪与解调，将视觉标记转变为鲁棒的机器人间光通信通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信虽普遍且简单，但存在固有局限：接收机器人难以定位信号来源、时钟同步困难、信号为广播而非定向传输。光通信作为互补通道，具有天然方向性和空间局部性，可弥补上述不足；而事件相机具备高时间分辨率、高动态范围和低延迟，适合作为高速光通信接收器。

Method: 提出异步跟踪与光通信（ATOC）系统，集成LED智能信标调制、基于事件的检测、光学跟踪与事件数据解调，形成单一处理流程，并支持同时跟踪与解调多个LED智能信标。

Result: 在系列实验室研究中验证了ATOC，并展示了两个应用：智慧城市演示与3D动作捕捉系统。

Conclusion: ATOC将传统视觉标记转化为鲁棒的通信通道，适用于多种高影响力机器人应用场景。

Abstract: Inter-robot communication is a key enabling technology in cooperative robotics applications. While wireless communication is ubiquitous, simple, and effective, it has inherent limitations: the receiving robot cannot easily localise the source of an incoming signal, clock synchronisation is challenging, and signals are broadcast to all nearby devices rather than targeted recipients. Optical communication provides a complementary channel that is inherently directional and spatially localised, directly addressing these limitations. Event cameras are bio-inspired dynamic vision sensors that respond to changes in image intensity with high temporal resolution, high dynamic range, and low latency, making them well-suited as receivers for high-rate optical communication in cooperative robotic systems. In this paper, we propose the Asynchronous Tracking and Optical Communication (ATOC) system, which integrates LED smart-beacon modulation, event-based detection, optical tracking, and event-data demodulation in a single pipeline. By simultaneously tracking and demodulating multiple LED smart beacons, ATOC transforms a conventional visual marker into a robust communication channel suitable for a wide range of high-impact robotics applications. We validate ATOC in a suite of laboratory studies and two 'applications': a smart-city demonstration and a 3D motion-capture system.

</details>


### [20] [TacOT: Learning Contact-Rich Dexterous Manipulation from Human Demonstrations via Tactile-Guided Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04363)
*Xingting Li,Yifan Han,Zijian Lin,Wei Hou,Chuqiao Lyu,Shoujie Li,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TacOT框架，利用触觉动力学引导最优传输，建立可靠的人-机器人示范对应关系，实现接触丰富的人到机器人灵巧操作技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类示范学习接触丰富的灵巧操作是可扩展的交互数据来源，但人-机器人对应关系不可靠，导致技能迁移困难。现有方法依赖视觉外观或运动相似性，可能把相似运动错误关联到不同的接触状态和力模式。

Method: TacOT利用动作-触觉动态时间规整（action-tactile DTW）识别人-机器人示范中交互动力学一致的对应关系，并用这些对应关系引导共享策略表示空间中的软最优传输对齐，无需预定义的帧级人-机器人配对。

Result: 在四个真实世界灵巧操作任务上，TacOT相比动作引导的OT在分布内任务上提升闭环成功率最多17个百分点，在针对性的人到机器人分布外迁移下提升20个百分点。分析表明触觉引导的对应选择了接触动力学更一致的示范对，并产生更好反映交互状态演化的潜在表示。

Conclusion: 触觉动力学为接触丰富的灵巧操作中建立可靠的人-机器人对应关系提供了有效的语义信号。

Abstract: Learning contact-rich dexterous manipulation from human demonstrations provides a scalable source of interaction data, yet transferring such skills to robots remains challenging due to unreliable human--robot correspondence. Existing human-to-robot transfer methods typically rely on visual appearance or motion similarity, which may associate similar motions with different contact states and force patterns. Tactile dynamics provide interaction-aware cues to distinguish manipulation processes with similar motions but different contact states. We introduce tactile-guided optimal transport (TacOT), a framework for human-to-robot contact-rich manipulation. TacOT leverages action--tactile dynamic time warping to identify human--robot demonstration correspondences with consistent interaction dynamics and uses these correspondences to guide soft optimal transport alignment in a shared policy representation space. This enables human demonstrations to provide contact-rich supervision for robot policy learning without requiring predefined frame-level human--robot pairing. Across four real-world dexterous manipulation tasks, TacOT improves closed-loop success rates over action-guided OT by up to 17 points on in-distribution tasks and 20 points under targeted human-to-robot out-of-distribution transfer. Further analyses show that tactile-guided correspondence selects demonstration pairs with more consistent contact dynamics and produces latent representations that better reflect interaction-state evolution. These results demonstrate that tactile dynamics provide an effective semantic signal for establishing reliable human-to-robot correspondence in contact-rich dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [21] [Human Behavior-Informed Crash Scenario Generation with Real-World Crash Priors for Autonomous Vehicle Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04366)
*Mingxing Peng,Xusen Guo,Long Chen,Xintao Yan,Siyu Teng,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出CrashSim，一个由人类行为驱动的碰撞场景生成框架，利用真实世界碰撞先验引导多智能体交通仿真，以更真实、可扩展地生成碰撞场景并评估自动驾驶安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶的可靠安全评估依赖于对罕见碰撞的真实仿真，但现有碰撞场景生成方法虽能提升碰撞发生率，却难以真实再现碰撞前的演化过程及真实世界中碰撞类型的分布。

Method: CrashSim使用来自真实世界碰撞的先验，捕捉碰撞前演化方式与碰撞类型分布，引导生成式多智能体交通仿真，使有限碰撞数据能够在自然驾驶场景下引导真实且可扩展的场景生成；并据此构建了nuCrash数据集。

Result: 与竞争方法相比，CrashSim更贴近真实世界的碰撞前行为、碰撞动力学、碰撞几何与碰撞类型分布。基于CrashSim构建的nuCrash数据集包含4000多个碰撞与近碰撞场景。对五种自动驾驶规划器的闭环评估显示，nuCrash比nuScenes更能揭示规划器安全能力差异。LLM辅助评估智能体进一步分析规划器失败，提供能力层面的诊断与针对性改进建议。

Conclusion: CrashSim实现了真实且可扩展的碰撞生成，从而支撑更具信息量的自动驾驶安全评估。

Abstract: Reliable safety evaluation of autonomous vehicles (AVs) is essential to improving road safety, yet it depends critically on realistic simulation of rare crashes. Existing crash scenario generation methods can increase collision occurrence, but often fail to realistically reproduce how crashes evolve before impact or the distribution of crash types observed in the real world. Here, we present CrashSim, a human behavior-informed crash scenario generation framework that uses real-world crash priors to guide generative multi-agent traffic simulation for more reliable AV safety evaluation. These priors capture how real-world crashes evolve before impact and how different crash types are distributed, allowing limited crash data to guide realistic and scalable scenario generation across naturalistic driving contexts. We evaluate CrashSim against competing methods, showing that it more closely reproduces real-world pre-impact behavior, collision dynamics, collision geometry and crash-type distributions. We further use CrashSim to construct nuCrash dataset, containing over 4,000 crash and near-crash scenarios. Closed-loop evaluation of five AV planners shows that nuCrash more effectively exposes differences in planner safety capabilities than nuScenes. An LLM-assisted evaluation agent further analyzes planner failures to provide capability-level diagnoses and targeted improvement guidance. Together, CrashSim enables realistic and scalable crash generation for more informative AV safety evaluation.

</details>


### [22] [Frame-Level Temporal Alignment for Human-to-Robot Visual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04372)
*Xizhe Zhang,Jingfeng Zhang,Zirun Zhou,Hong Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FLTA框架，利用全局进度先验与局部时序顺序先验，实现无需帧级对应标注的人-机器人视觉表征对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人类视频预训练的视觉表征迁移到机器人操作，需要学习人-机器人演示之间的可靠对应关系。但配对演示在执行速率和不反映任务进度的非关键帧比例上存在差异，导致相同相对时间戳的帧可能对应不同任务阶段，使对应学习失败。

Method: FLTA使用两个时序先验：全局进度先验将归一化时间位置与视觉相似度结合，构建软对应目标；局部时序顺序先验惩罚倒退转换，允许停留和变化的前进速率以适配执行速率差异。方法无需帧级对应标注即可学习共享的任务进度表征。

Result: 在ResNet-50与ViT编码器上，平均仿真成功率相对最佳基线分别提升46.93%和65.96%，并在真实机器人操作任务上取得更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 有效的人-机器人适配更多取决于选择更新哪些参数，而非更新参数的数量。

Abstract: Transferring visual representations pretrained on human videos to robot manipulation requires learning reliable correspondences between human and robot demonstrations. However, paired demonstrations can differ in execution rate and in the proportion of non-key frames that do not directly reflect task progress. Frames at the same relative timestamp may therefore represent different task stages, which can cause correspondence learning to fail. To address these issues, we propose Frame-Level Temporal Alignment (FLTA), a framework that uses two temporal priors to adapt visual encoders pretrained on human videos for robot manipulation. It learns shared task-progress representations without frame-level correspondence annotations, allowing frames at different relative temporal positions to match. A global progress prior combines normalized temporal positions with visual similarity to construct a soft correspondence target. A local temporal order prior penalizes backward transitions while allowing stays and varying forward rates to accommodate execution-rate differences. With ResNet-50 and ViT encoders, our method achieves relative improvements of 46.93% and 65.96%, respectively, over the best baselines in average simulation success rates and achieves higher task success rates on real-world manipulation tasks. These results also suggest that effective human-robot adaptation depends less on the number of parameters updated than on which parameters are selected. Our project page is available at https://rtx5090ultra.github.io/FLTA-Project-Page/.

</details>


### [23] [AgenticTactileVLA: Contact-Guided Execution-Time Supervision for Generalizable Dexterous Manipulation without VLA Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04391)
*Elizaveta Semenyakina,Ivan Snegirev,Mikhail Kiselev,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Artem Lykov,Hajira Amjad,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AgenticTactileVLA 执行期监督器，利用指位与电机力矩反馈实现物体级自适应抓取控制，在固定 VLA 基础上显著提升未见物体完成率且无需触觉传感器或重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽能预测可迁移的操作策略，但在实际物体上执行不可靠：同一抓取方式兼容的物体在几何与柔顺性上存在差异，且闭合遮挡会使视觉反馈退化。因此需要把部分物体特异的适应从"预测阶段"转移到"物理交互阶段"。

Method: 以固定 VLA 提供接近与手部目标，AgenticTactileVLA 作为执行期监督器，决定是否保持透明、微调手指屈曲、保留或释放修正后的构型、将控制权交回 VLA 重试，或选择柔顺手部控制模式。该方法以手指位置与电机力矩反馈作为本体感受式接触证据，不需要触觉传感器，也不需要重训练 VLA。在 Unitree G1 配 BrainCo Revo2 手上进行实验。

Result: 在五个未参与 VLA 训练的物体上进行随机匹配方块评测：基础 VLA 完成率 61.3%，无条件"闭合至堵转"控制 72.0%，监督器为 84.0%（共享预算下）；增益在每个物体上均为正，并在中等位姿扰动下保持。消融显示增益并非仅由延长闭合解释；选择性触发将修正次数减少 65.7% 且完成率无明显变化。保留审计显示接受可预测 88.9% 的保留；柔顺物体暴露出保守的误拒。薄壁杯实验证明可上下文路由至柔顺控制，效果与始终柔顺的参考相当。

Conclusion: 接触引导的执行期自适应可在不进行物体特异重训练的前提下，通过调整物理实现方式来提升固定 VLA 对未见物体的物体级泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies may predict a transferable manipulation strategy yet fail to realize it reliably on the encountered object: objects compatible with the same grasp differ in geometry and compliance, and visual feedback degrades under closure occlusion. AgenticTactileVLA is presented as an execution-time supervisor that shifts part of object-specific adaptation from prediction to physical interaction. A fixed VLA provides the approach and hand targets; the supervisor decides whether to remain transparent, refine finger flexion, retain or release the corrected configuration, return control to the VLA for retry, or select a compliant hand-control regime. It uses finger-position and motor-effort feedback as proprioceptive contact evidence and requires neither tactile sensors nor VLA retraining. On a Unitree G1 with a BrainCo Revo2 hand, a randomized matched-block evaluation on five objects held out from VLA training yields 61.3% completion for the base VLA, 72.0% for unconditional close-to-stall control, and 84.0% for the supervisor under a shared budget; the gain is positive on every object and persists under moderate pose perturbations. Ablations show the gain is not explained by extended closure alone, and that selective triggering reduces correction episodes by 65.7% with no detected change in completion. A retention audit shows acceptance predicts retention in 88.9% of held-out cases, while compliant objects expose conservative false rejection. A thin-walled-cup study demonstrates contextual routing to compliant control, matching an always-compliant reference. These results suggest that contact-guided execution-time adaptation can improve the object-level generalization of a fixed VLA to held-out objects by adapting physical realization without object-specific retraining.

</details>


### [24] [Reachability-Guided Sequential Quadratic Programming-Guarded Model Predictive Path Integral for Safe Nonlinear Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04406)
*Alexandre Didier,Jason J. Choi,Namhoon Cho,Claire J. Tomlin,Melanie N. Zeilinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReSQ-MPPI，用离线 HJR 价值函数引导 MPPI 采样，再用少量 SQP 迭代在全阶 MPC 中细化，兼顾安全与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全机器人控制需同时兼顾长时域性能优化与硬性状态/输入约束，但现有方法只能部分解决该矛盾：采样型 MPC（如 MPPI）擅处理非线性非凸问题，却因有限采样和无约束加权平均更新可能输出不安全控制；确定性非线性 MPC 可显式处理约束，但实时安全性与性能高度依赖热启动和局部收敛；HJR 能提供严格安全证书，但离线价值函数计算仅对降阶模型可行。

Method: ReSQ-MPPI 采用可达性引导的"生成—细化"架构：离线对降阶模型计算 HJR 价值函数，在线引导 MPPI 采样趋向安全且有前景的轨迹候选；随后在完整阶 MPC 问题中用少量序贯二次规划（SQP）迭代细化该解。关键观察是标准 MPPI 推断步为无约束加权最小二乘问题，ReSQ-MPPI 将其替换为约束 MPC 细化，在可行时恢复 MPPI 更新，否则做最小修正。

Result: 在杂乱导航与自动驾驶赛车环境仿真中，ReSQ-MPPI 相比独立 MPPI、MPC 以及可达性过滤的采样控制基线，在安全性与性能上均有提升。

Conclusion: 通过融合 HJR 的严格安全引导与 MPPI 的采样优势，并以约束 SQP 细化保证可行性，ReSQ-MPPI 有效统一了长时域性能优化与硬约束安全，验证了可达性引导生成结合约束细化的有效性。

Abstract: Safe robot control often requires combining long-horizon performance optimization with hard state and input constraints, but existing approaches tend to address this tradeoff partially. Sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) methods such as model predictive path integral (MPPI) are effective in handling nonlinear and nonconvex environments, yet their finite-sample rollouts and unconstrained weighted-average update can return an unsafe control. Deterministic nonlinear MPC can explicitly incorporate constraints, but its real-time safety and performance depends strongly on warm starts and local convergence. Hamilton--Jacobi reachability (HJR) provides rigorous safety certificates, but offline value-function computation remains practical only for reduced-order models. We propose ReSQ-MPPI, a reachability-informed generation--refinement architecture that combines these complementary strengths. An HJR value function computed offline for a reduced-order model guides online MPPI sampling toward safe, promising trajectory candidates. The MPPI solution is then refined by a small number of sequential quadratic programming (SQP) iterations in a full-order MPC problem. A key observation is that the standard MPPI inference step is an unconstrained weighted least-squares problem; ReSQ-MPPI replaces it with a constrained MPC refinement that recovers the MPPI update when it is feasible and minimally modifies it otherwise. Simulations in cluttered navigation and autonomous racing environments demonstrate that ReSQ-MPPI improves safety and performance over standalone MPPI, MPC, and reachability-filtered sampling-based control baselines.

</details>


### [25] [WasserMan: Benchmark for Underwater Manipulation Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04536)
*Danil Belov,Artem Erkhov,Sergei Parsegov,Pavel Osinenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WasserMan——首个面向浮基水下接触操作视觉运动学习的多任务仿真基准，含10个专家可解任务、两种载臂平台、双臂配置与水下动力学，并给出ACT、DP、BC及SmolVLA的对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下操作需同时耦合视觉决策、接触力与推进器控制的浮动基座，但此前缺乏面向浮基水下接触操作视觉运动学习的多任务仿真基准，难以系统比较策略的可行性与执行开销。

Method: 构建WasserMan基准：提供10个专家可解任务、两种载臂平台、双臂配置与水下动力学；其中9个任务带学习策略评估。在6个任务上以3次训练、等量采样窗口预算对比ACT、扩散策略（DP）与行为克隆，并在3个任务上加入预训练SmolVLA评估。

Result: 在测试设置下，移除积分作用可能阻止任务完成；改变动作接口会在专家回放成功的前提下降低学习策略成功率；水流对完成度与驱动开销产生依赖任务的影响。

Conclusion: 带版本管理的任务、演示与逐回合证据支持一种将专家可行性、学习完成度与执行开销相分离的评估协议，为水下视觉运动操作研究提供可复现的多任务基准。

Abstract: Underwater manipulation couples visual decisions, contact forces and a thruster-controlled floating base. We introduce WasserMan, to our knowledge the first multi-task simulation benchmark for visuomotor learning of floating-base underwater contact manipulation. It provides ten expert-solvable tasks, two vehicle-arm platforms, a bimanual configuration and underwater dynamics. Nine tasks have learned-policy evaluations. We compare ACT, diffusion policies (DP) and behavioral cloning on six tasks, with three training runs and equal sampled-window budgets. Pretrained SmolVLA adds evaluations on three tasks. In the tested settings, removing integral action can prevent completion, while changing action interfaces can reduce learned-policy success despite successful expert replay. Currents produce task-dependent changes in completion and actuation effort. Versioned tasks, demonstrations and per-episode evidence support a protocol separating expert feasibility, learned completion and execution effort.

</details>


### [26] [DreamFormer: Dream Imitation with a Transformer World Model for Language-Conditioned Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04540)
*Mostafa Kotb,Cornelius Weber,Muhammad Burhan Hafez,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamFormer：在世界模型潜想象中模仿专家演示的基于模型的语言条件多任务智能体，远距离CALVIN任务性能超越同类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线行为克隆存在协变量偏移（covariate shift），且此前Transformer世界模型的多token表示使长距离想象难以承受，需要更高效、可迁移的建模方式。

Method: 先用无结构玩要数据学习任务无关的Transformer世界模型；再在与专家演示于潜空间对齐的内在奖励下，在想象中在线优化策略，使其经历自身错误；并将高分辨率多视图机器人观测编码为单个输入token，避免空间下采样与多token表示。

Result: CALVIN基准上，单环境评估平均完成2.52个任务/五步链，优于LUMOS的2.34；零样本迁移到未见环境性能1.30，接近HULC行为克隆基线0.67的两倍，且想象推演仍可行。

Conclusion: 潜想象内在线训练缓解了离线行为克隆的协变量偏移；世界模型学到的动力学比直接克隆的策略更易迁移，与生物智能体中内部模型支持新情境行为的作用一致。

Abstract: We introduce DreamFormer, a model-based agent that acquires language-conditioned, multi-task skills by imitating expert demonstrations within the latent imagination of a learned world model. DreamFormer first learns a task-agnostic Transformer world model from unstructured play data, then acquires task-specific behaviors by optimizing an intrinsic reward that aligns agent-generated rollouts with expert demonstrations in latent space. Since the policy is trained on-policy inside imagination, it is exposed to its own errors during training, mitigating the covariate shift inherent to offline behavioral cloning. To make long-horizon imagination affordable, DreamFormer encodes a high-resolution multi-view robotic observation into a single input token, avoiding both spatial downsampling and the multi-token representations used by prior Transformer world models. On the long-horizon CALVIN benchmark, DreamFormer outperforms LUMOS, the comparable model-based agent, on single-environment evaluation (2.52 vs 2.34 average tasks completed per chain of five) while keeping imagination rollouts tractable. Against HULC, the behavior cloning baseline, it nearly doubles performance on zero-shot transfer to an unseen environment (1.30 vs 0.67), indicating that dynamics learned by the world model transfer more readily than a directly cloned policy. This is consistent with the role attributed to internal models in biological agents, where a model of environment dynamics supports behavior in situations not previously encountered.

</details>


### [27] [NM-LIO: Multiple LiDAR-Inertial Odometry Addressing LiDAR Measurement Noise Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04551)
*Gunhee Shin,Seungjae Lee,Minho Oh,Dongkyu Lee,Jaeyoung Lee,Youngwoo Seo,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出噪声感知的多激光雷达惯性里程计（NM-LIO），通过建模各雷达测量噪声并估计残差不确定性，解决多雷达间噪声差异问题，在公开数据集上实现了更精确的里程计估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多激光雷达惯性里程计因精度和可靠性高而被广泛应用，但不同激光雷达之间测量噪声存在差异。现有方法通常忽略这些噪声差异，从而显著影响定位精度。

Method: 引入噪声模型量化每个激光雷达的测量噪声；基于测量噪声估计残差的不确定性，使测量模型能够捕捉噪声差异，从而构建噪声感知的多激光雷达惯性里程计（NM-LIO）。

Result: 在公开的多激光雷达数据集上进行评估，并与当前最先进方法对比，结果表明该方法通过考虑噪声差异，能在多种环境中准确估计里程计。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模和利用多激光雷达间的噪声差异，可有效提升多激光雷达惯性里程计的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multiple LiDAR-inertial odometry methods have been widely applied in robotic applications owing to their enhanced accuracy and reliability. However, adopting multiple LiDAR systems can be challenging due to the discrepancies in measurement noise across different LiDARs. Existing methods have typically overlooked the noise discrepancies, which can significantly affect accuracy. In this paper, we propose Noise-aware Multiple LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (NM-LIO) that addresses the noise discrepancies. We integrate a noise model to quantify the measurement noise of each LiDAR. Additionally, we estimate the uncertainty of the residuals based on the measurement noise, allowing the measurement model to capture the noise discrepancies. Our proposed method is evaluated on a public multiple LiDAR dataset and compared with state-of-the-art methods. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method can accurately estimate the odometry in various environments by accounting for the noise discrepancies.

</details>


### [28] [Online Target-less Radar-LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration via Joint Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04552)
*Gunhee Shin,Yunsoo Kim,Chanhyuk Lee,Wanhee Kim,Minwoo Lee,Sungwoo Han,Jeongwoo Woo,Hyuntai Chin,Minha Park,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向雷达-激光雷达-相机系统的在线无标靶联合外参标定框架，通过对所有传感器对的残差联合优化，并结合自适应雷达噪声滤波与对应点累积策略，在多场景自采数据集上降低了各传感器对的标定误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达、激光雷达与相机的融合感知性能高度依赖三者之间的精确外参标定。现有无标靶方法多针对单一传感器对设计，将两两标定结果组合难以保证三传感器间的一致性；同时雷达测量稀疏且噪声大，使得涉及雷达的传感器对标定不可靠。

Method: 提出联合标定框架：为每个传感器对构建残差，并对外参进行整体联合优化以最小化总残差。此外引入自适应雷达噪声滤波器，利用随距离变化的余量剔除虚假雷达回波；并提出对应点累积策略，将稀疏的雷达对应关系跨帧聚合。

Result: 在覆盖多种城市环境的自采雷达-激光雷达-相机数据集上验证，相比当前最先进的相机-激光雷达基线方法，所提方法在全部传感器对上均降低了标定误差。

Conclusion: 联合优化三传感器外参可解决两两标定的不一致性问题，配合雷达噪声滤波与跨帧对应点累积能有效提升含雷达传感器对的标定可靠性，实现稳健的在线无标靶三传感器外参标定。

Abstract: Fusing radar, LiDAR, and camera enables robust perception in diverse and adverse conditions, but the fusion performance critically depends on accurate extrinsic calibration among the three sensors. In this paper, we address the problem of online target-less extrinsic calibration for the radar-LiDAR-camera system. Existing target-less methods are mostly designed for a single sensor pair, and composing the pairwise results does not guarantee consistency across the three sensors. Moreover, the sparse and noisy radar measurements make the radar-involving pairs unreliable. To tackle these challenges, we propose a joint calibration framework that constructs residuals for each sensor pair and optimizes the extrinsics of all pairs together to minimize the overall residual. Furthermore, we introduce an adaptive radar noise filter that rejects spurious radar returns using a range-dependent margin, and a correspondence accumulation strategy that aggregates sparse radar correspondences over frames. We validate our method on an in-house radar-LiDAR-camera dataset covering diverse urban environments, where it reduces calibration errors across all sensor pairs over a state-of-the-art camera-LiDAR baseline.

</details>


### [29] [Leveraging Cost-effective Robotics for K-12 STEM Education through Water Quality Monitoring Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04565)
*Rishi Mukherjee,Andrew Ruiz,Travis Henderson,Resha Tejpaul,Kris Simonson,David Mulla,Brian McNeil,Nikolaos Papanikolopoulos,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Jar Jar ROV——一款低成本、开源的遥控水下航行器平台，用于中学生开展公民科学水质监测，并通过覆盖美国某州100多名学生的大规模部署验证了其教育效果与科学产出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让K-12学生参与真实的科学探究仍具挑战，尤其环境科学与机器人交叉领域。该研究旨在通过低成本开源ROV平台，赋能中学生开展水质监测实践，弥合课堂教学与真实科研之间的鸿沟。

Method: 设计并开发了Jar Jar ROV低成本开源水下遥控平台，随后在美国一个州开展大规模部署，100多名中学生完成了ROV的搭建、编程并在当地湖泊中实际投放使用；通过导师评分与项目评估收集教育与科学数据。

Result: 学生在动手活动中参与度高，ROV搭建环节获得导师的满分平均评价；项目建立了基层监测网络，累计产出近11000条经过验证的温度、pH、溶解氧和浊度测量数据。但评估发现存在"参与度缺口"，在电子组装和数据上传等复杂任务中，学生兴趣急剧下降。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个经过验证、可扩展的将机器人技术融入环境教育的模型，并给出数据驱动的改进路线图，未来改进将聚焦降低技术门槛、建立数据采集与科学发现之间更直观的联系，从而更好地赋能下一代公民科学家。

Abstract: Engaging K-12 students in authentic scientific research remains a significant challenge, particularly at the intersection of environmental science and robotics. We introduce the Jar Jar ROV, a low-cost, open-source Remotely Operated Vehicle (ROV) platform designed for citizen science-based water quality monitoring by middle school students. This paper presents the design of the platform and the results of a large-scale deployment with over 100 students across a US state who built, programmed, and deployed the ROVs in local lakes. The educational framework yielded high student engagement in hands-on activities, with ROV construction earning a perfect average score from mentors. Scientifically, the program established a grassroots monitoring network, generating nearly eleven thousand validated measurements of temperature, pH, dissolved oxygen, and turbidity. However, our evaluation identified a critical "engagement gap," with student interest declining sharply during more complex tasks such as electronics assembly and data uploading. This paper contributes both a validated, scalable model for integrating robotics into environmental education and a data-driven roadmap for future improvements. These enhancements focus on lowering technical barriers and creating a more intuitive link between data collection and scientific discovery, addressing a key challenge in empowering the next generation of citizen scientists.

</details>


### [30] [RiskFly: Frustum-Aligned Spatio-Temporal Risk Fields for One-Stage Agile Flight in Dynamic Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04566)
*Luxia Ai,Haopeng Chen,Yuchao Mei,Guohao Zhang,Wenbing Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RiskFly 是一种单阶段学习型规划器，通过与动作空间对齐的时空风险场预测，实现未知动态环境中的敏捷飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单阶段学习型规划器虽快速且无需专家示范，但其编码器仅接收标量轨迹代价，缺乏空间与时间结构，导致避障退化为晚期反应式机动。因此需要让规划器明确知道危险在何处、何时出现。

Method: 采用双流观测：短深度序列与视锥对齐的倒置球面距离图序列，其角度单元与末端状态候选一一对应。辅助头回归视锥对齐的时空风险场，由特权最近点接近（CPA）目标监督。该自预测风险场在五次多项式轨迹的到达时刻被可微查询，并同时进入训练目标，使表征监督与规划梯度在同一空间汇合。部署时丢弃特权信号，仅用机载深度与本体感知，无需地图。

Result: 在大量仿真和资源受限平台上的零样本真实飞行中，在端到端延迟相当的情况下取得了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 将风险预测与动作空间对齐，可让单阶段规划器在无需地图与特权信号的情况下实现更安全、更敏捷的飞行。

Abstract: Agile flight in unknown, cluttered, and dynamic environments requires a planner that knows where and when danger will appear, not only that a trajectory is dangerous. One-stage learning-based planners trained with differentiable privileged costs are fast and expert-free, but the only signal reaching their encoder is a scalar trajectory cost with no spatial or temporal structure, so avoidance degrades into late reactive maneuvers. We present RiskFly, a one-stage planner that predicts risk in the same space in which it acts. A dual-stream observation pairs a short depth sequence with a frustum-aligned inverted spherical range-map sequence, whose angular cells match the end-state proposals one to one. An auxiliary head regresses a frustum-aligned spatio-temporal risk field, supervised by a privileged closest-point-of-approach (CPA) target. This self-predicted field is queried differentiably along the instantiated quintic trajectory at its own arrival times, and also enters the training objective, so representation supervision and planning gradients meet in a single space. Privileged signals are discarded at deployment, and the planner runs map-free from onboard depth and proprioception. Extensive simulation and zero-shot real-world flights on resource-constrained platforms show higher success rates at comparable end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [31] [ForeAct3D: Policy-Grounded Future World Modeling for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04607)
*Zhe Tao,Feiran Wang,Gaowen Liu,Ramana Rao Kompella$,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForeAct3D 提出一种在 VLA 策略内部进行"策略锚定"的未来世界建模框架，通过可学习几何查询解码语义 3D 场景状态并施加物理一致性约束，在无需机器人预训练的情况下显著提升操作任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略虽能从共享特征预测未来观测，但预测与策略实际执行的动作解耦，且对场景演化缺乏物理约束，导致动作与结果之间的因果联系不明确。

Method: 利用可学习几何查询从策略表征中解码深度、语义分割和相机位姿，形成当前与未来的语义 3D 场景状态；未来查询以策略生成的动作块为条件以锚定预测；通过背景静态性与实例级刚性的物理一致性闭合联系两个状态，并将腕部相机位姿锚定到末端执行器运动学；这些目标在训练时塑造共享表征，推理时无需未来预测。

Result: 无机器人预训练下，LIBERO 平均成功率 98.3%，CALVIN 平均任务长度 3.73，均超越基策略；消融显示语义 3D 监督、物理一致性和动作条件各自提升操作性能，动作条件显著改善未来物体定位；真实世界空间放置、物体插入和顺序操作任务平均成功率从 6.7% 提升至 37.8%。

Conclusion: 将未来世界建模与策略动作紧密结合、并施加几何与物理约束，能有效改善 VLA 策略的表征质量与下游操作性能，且不增加推理开销、无需机器人预训练。

Abstract: Robots need to anticipate how their actions will change the world, since manipulation success hinges on the resulting contacts and object motions. However, existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies that predict future observations from shared features leave the forecast decoupled from the actions the policy will actually execute, and impose no physical constraints on how the scene may evolve. We introduce ForeAct3D, a framework for policy-grounded future world modeling within VLA policies. Learnable geometric queries decode depth, semantic segmentation, and camera pose from the policy representation into current and future semantic 3D scene states, and the future queries are conditioned on the policy-generated action chunk to ground the forecast in the planned interaction. A physical-consistency closure relates the two states through background staticity and instance-level rigidity, and anchors the wrist-camera pose to end-effector kinematics. These objectives shape the shared representation used for action generation during training, and no future prediction is required at inference. Without robot pretraining, ForeAct3D achieves 98.3\% average success on LIBERO and an average task length of 3.73 on CALVIN, outperforming its base policy on every suite. Ablations show that semantic 3D supervision, physical consistency, and action conditioning each improve manipulation performance, and that action conditioning substantially improves future object localization. Real-world experiments on spatial placement, object insertion, and sequential manipulation further raise average success from 6.7\% to 37.8\% over the base policy. The project page and code are available at https://github.com/anthonytao80-crypto/ForeAct3D.

</details>


### [32] [Exploiting Hierarchical Controller Structure in Contextual Parameter Learning for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04609)
*Sebastian Hirt,Lukas Theiner,Jan Peters,Rolf Findeisen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种上下文贝叶斯优化框架，用于分层控制系统跨层参数的联合学习，并在人形机器人推箱任务中取得最低经验遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层控制架构广泛用于机器人与复杂控制问题，其闭环性能依赖分布于各层的参数。独立调参忽略跨层交互，需联合优化。

Method: 不将闭环性能仅建模为标量黑箱，而是保留任务性能、实现质量与控制代价的分别观测；用相关多输出高斯过程建模这些分量，并对已知的聚合方式解析求值；利用分层控制带来的三类特性：性能信息、跨层参数耦合、随工况变化的关系。

Result: 在人形机器人移动操作（推箱）任务上，联合调节质心预测控制器与全身控制器，适应不同箱子质量，训练与自适应阶段均取得最低平均经验遗憾。

Conclusion: 上下文贝叶斯优化可有效实现分层控制系统的跨层联合参数学习，优于所比较的基线方法。

Abstract: Hierarchical control architectures are widely used to decompose complex control problems into interacting control levels and are particularly important in robotics, where planning, whole-body motion, and lower-level control must be coordinated across different levels of abstraction and time scales. Their overall closed-loop performance, however, depends strongly on parameters distributed across the hierarchy, such that tuning controllers on different levels independently may neglect relevant cross-layer interactions. We propose a contextual Bayesian optimization framework for joint parameter learning in hierarchical control systems. Rather than modeling closed-loop performance only as a scalar black-box function, we retain separate observations of task performance, realization quality, and control effort. A correlated multi-output Gaussian process models these performance components, while their known aggregation into the overall closed-loop objective is evaluated analytically. The formulation exploits three complementary consequences of hierarchical control: informative performance quantities exposed by the hierarchy, coupling between parameters of different controller levels, and variations of these relations with operating conditions. We evaluate the approach for humanoid loco-manipulation, jointly tuning a centroidal predictive controller and a whole-body controller for physical box pushing under varying box mass. The proposed method achieves the lowest mean empirical regret during both training and adaptation among the considered baselines.

</details>


### [33] [An End-to-End Framework for Modelling Pneumatic Soft Robots Based on Differentiable Finite Element Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04612)
*Shaohong Zhong,Yao Yao,Perla Maiolino,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端可微分有限元（FEM）框架，用于气动软体机器人的建模与系统辨识，自动将CAD转为体素化四面体粘弹性网格，结合点云数据辨识材料与驱动参数，并支持轨迹优化，实验位置误差小于3mm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人因非线性、复杂动力学和复杂几何形状，建模困难，系统辨识与动力学建模不准确，限制了其在精确机器人任务中的应用；现有建模方法在精度、计算效率和速度之间存在权衡。

Method: 提出端到端可微分FEM框架：自动将计算机辅助设计（CAD）转换为体素化四面体粘弹性FEM网格，准确表示几何与驱动机制；将易于获取的点云数据与可微分FEM结合，以最小实验设置辨识材料参数和动态驱动参数；利用机器人动力学与接触交互的梯度进行轨迹优化。

Result: 在复杂波纹管状气动软体机器人上验证框架，建模精度高，最大位置误差小于3mm，并成功应用于路径跟随和抓取等真实世界运动规划任务。

Conclusion: 可微分FEM框架在精度、效率和速度之间取得良好平衡，能生成可靠的软体机器人模型，支持下游精确任务，具有良好的实际应用效果。

Abstract: Soft robots present significant modelling challenges due to their non-linearity, complex dynamics and potentially intricate geometries. These difficulties in accurate system identification and dynamics modelling limit their applications in precise robotics tasks. Prior modelling approaches typically suffer from trade-offs in accuracy, computational efficiency, or speed. The differentiable finite element method (FEM) offers a promising balance between these desiderata for soft robot modelling, and enables the use of gradients for efficient calibration and trajectory optimisation. In this paper, we propose an end-to-end differentiable FEM-based framework designed to streamline modelling and system identification for pneumatic soft robots, enabling the generation of reliable models for downstream tasks. The framework automates the conversion of computer-aided designs into voxelised tetrahedral viscoelastic FEM meshes that accurately represent the robot's geometry and actuation mechanism. By integrating easily acquired point cloud data with differentiable FEM, the framework achieves precise identification of material and dynamic actuation parameters using a minimal experimental setup. Additionally, the differentiable nature of the model facilitates trajectory optimisation by leveraging gradients from robot dynamics and contact interactions. We validate the framework by modelling a complex bellow-shaped pneumatic soft robot and demonstrate its efficacy in real-world motion planning tasks. Experimental results indicate high modelling accuracy, with a maximum positional error of less than 3 mm, and successful application in tasks such as path following and grasping.

</details>


### [34] [PerturBot: Breaking Shortcut Priors in Vision-Language-Action Models with Perturbative Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04616)
*Mingyu Liu,Chonghao Sima,Tianjian Feng,Hanqing Wang,Cong Chen,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Perturbot训练框架与GroundingFscore离线诊断指标，缓解VLA策略对"模态捷径"的依赖，促使决策依赖任务证据而非训练中的偶发规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在完成复杂任务时会忽略本应决定动作的证据：腕部相机附近的物体可挤占指令目标；熟悉名词可触发训练配对的操作（即使动词改变）；空夹爪仍会抬起。作者将这类视觉、词汇、运动线索足以预测专家动作而无需任务证据的规律称为"模态捷径"。更多同类示范可提升成功率却保留捷径，因此需要新的训练与评估视角。

Method: Perturbot：1) 任务保持的腕部视角扰动；2) 用决策相关描述丰富指令；3) 加入随机与失败轨迹片段并按其所含行为重新标注。该方法不改变推理阶段。同时提出GroundingFscore，用于离线诊断策略对模态捷径的依赖程度。

Result: GroundingFscore可诊断策略依赖模态捷径的严重程度；任务成功率反映策略是否提升，而GroundingFscore揭示策略扩展是否"健康"（是否依赖任务证据而非捷径）。

Conclusion: Perturbot与GroundingFscore共同构成训练与评估框架，在保持对任务相关证据响应性的同时，将VLA决策与捷径先验解耦，作为数据规模扩展的补充。

Abstract: A vision--language--action (VLA) policy can complete complex tasks while ignoring the evidence that should determine its actions. An object held near the wrist camera can displace the instructed target. Language and action show the same pattern: a familiar noun can trigger the operation it was paired with in training even after the verb changes, and a gripper that closed on nothing may lift anyway. We call these dependencies modality shortcuts: regularities in successful demonstrations make visual, lexical, or motor cues sufficient to predict expert actions without the task evidence needed for the underlying decision. More demonstrations of the same kind can raise task success while leaving these shortcuts intact. We propose Perturbot which makes task-relevant evidence easier to use and shortcuts insufficient on their own: it applies task-preserving wrist-view perturbations, enriches instructions with decision-relevant captions, and adds random and failed trajectory segments relabeled with the behavior they contain. It complements scaling by changing what is scaled, and leaves inference unchanged. Moreover, we propose GroundingFscore, an offline score that diagnoses how severely a policy relies on modality shortcuts. Task success rate shows whether a policy improves, while GroundingFscore reveals whether the policy scales healthily, relying on task evidence rather than shortcuts. Together, Perturbot and GroundingFscore provide a training-and-evaluation framework for disentangling VLA decisions from shortcut priors while preserving responsiveness to task-relevant evidence.

</details>


### [35] [RoboIRS: Inference-Time Internal Representation Steering for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04681)
*Jiuzhou Lei,Chang Liu,Dayou Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Xiao Liang,Yu She,Zhiwen Fan,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboIRS，一种推理时内部表示引导方法，通过成功与失败轨迹训练线性分类器、定位与结果相关的干预位置并推导任务特定引导方向，在冻结策略参数下提升分布外任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）与世界-动作模型（WAMs）在分布外任务变化下性能常显著下降，尽管仍保留部分任务能力，需要一种不更新策略参数即可恢复该能力的方法。

Method: RoboIRS 属于推理时内部表示引导方法：利用成功与失败的 rollout 数据训练线性分类器，据此选择与任务结果相关的干预位置，并推导任务特定的引导方向，从而在不更新策略参数的情况下对内部表示进行引导。

Result: 在 15 个仿真任务上、使用冻结的 π0.5 策略，平均成功率从 44.4% 提升至 66.2%，优于其他推理时干预基线且推理时间增加很少；在真实机器人操作中验证有效，并适用于世界-动作模型 Cosmos Policy，平均成功率从 35.4% 提升至 55.4%。

Conclusion: 直接引导机器人策略的内部表示可以在推理阶段提升策略性能，说明推理时表示引导是恢复分布外任务能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models (WAMs) often degrade under out-of-distribution task variations despite retaining partial task capability. To recover such capability, we propose RoboIRS, an inference-time internal representation steering method that uses successful and failed rollouts to train linear classifiers, select outcome-relevant intervention locations, and derive task-specific steering directions without updating policy parameters. On 15 simulation tasks with a frozen $π0.5$ policy, RoboIRS improves the average success rate from 44.4% to 66.2%, outperforming alternative inference-time intervention baselines while adding little inference time. We further validate RoboIRS on real-robot manipulation using the same $π0.5$ policy and demonstrate its applicability to a world-action model Cosmos Policy, where the average success rate improves from 35.4% to 55.4%. These results show that directly steering internal robot-policy representations can improve the performance of robot policies at inference time. Project website is available at https://rollingoat.github.io/roboirs/.

</details>


### [36] [Test-Time Training as Residual Memory for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04701)
*Haoxuan Wang,Gengyu Zhang,Ramana Rao Kompella,Gaowen Liu,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TTT-RM，将测试时训练(TTT)重定位为"残差记忆"，专门存储无法从当前上下文恢复的历史信息，与有界记忆库互补，提升长时程机器人操作的记忆可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作依赖历史信息，但完整保留历史代价高昂。现有方法或存选取的观测于有界记忆库，或用TTT将历史压进固定参数状态，但都没区分"已能从当前上下文恢复的信息"和"必须额外保留的信息"。

Method: TTT-RM学习一个历史解码器，从当前观测和检索到的记忆重建历史表示；重建残差即为当前上下文无法解释的部分，作为TTT的学习目标。TTT慢权重针对该残差目标优化，使在线快权重更新编码互补的历史信息；再用快权重状态生成残差记忆表示，用于条件化动作生成。

Result: 在记忆密集型仿真基准和真实任务上，TTT-RM在多种记忆设计下均持续提升，优于多个基线，并支持三分钟、八阶段收拾任务的持续执行。

Conclusion: 通过显式区分可恢复与不可恢复的历史信息，将TTT作为有界记忆库的残差补充，能有效缓解长时程记忆的扩展性问题。

Abstract: Memory is essential for long-horizon robotic manipulation, where successful actions may depend on past events that are no longer recoverable from the current observation. As episodes grow longer, however, retaining the full history becomes increasingly costly, creating a fundamental scalability challenge for memory-augmented policies. Existing approaches address this challenge by either storing selected past observations in a bounded memory bank or compressing interaction history into a fixed-size parametric state through Test-Time Training (TTT). Yet these formulations do not explicitly distinguish between historical information that can already be recovered from the policy's current context and information that must persist beyond it. We introduce TTT-RM, which repurposes TTT as Residual Memory, using fast weights not to generically compress history but to complement a bounded memory bank by preserving task-relevant historical information that cannot be recovered from the policy's current context. Concretely, TTT-RM learns a history decoder that reconstructs historical representations from the current observation and retrieved memory. The resulting reconstruction residual captures what this context fails to explain and serves as the learning target for TTT. The TTT slow weights are optimized against this residual target so that online fast-weight updates learn to encode complementary historical information over time. The fast-weight state is then queried to produce a residual memory representation that conditions action generation. Extensive experiments on memory-intensive simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks show that TTT-RM consistently improves across multiple memory designs, outperforms diverse baselines, and supports sustained execution on a three-minute, eight-stage stowing task.

</details>


### [37] [Emergent Underactuation in Fully Actuated Multirotors by Minimizing a Homogeneous Allocation Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04726)
*Antonio Franchi,Barbara Bazzana*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"互补原理"：当只给定位置轨迹时，对正齐次分配代价最小化会让全驱动多旋翼表现出类欠驱动行为；而全驱动在扰动抑制与瞬态力生成上仍有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
