Result: 提出了多项缓解策略，如基于GNSS定时和NTP协议的系统级时间同步、面向不同传感器配置的定制标定流程，以及提升现场部署可靠性和数据完整性的设计实践，从而增强自主车辆测试的可复现性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该经验总结可作为开发新型多传感器系统的研究人员的参考，指导未来平台设计，提升自主驾驶测试的可复现性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper reports on the challenges encountered and lessons learned during the development and deployment of a flexible multi-sensor platform for autonomous driving research. It aims to serve as a reference for researchers developing new multi-sensor systems. As the need for reliable, diverse datasets increases, novel environments and sensing configurations are essential to tackle real-world operational challenges. Consequently, many research groups create custom multi-sensory data collection platforms, where fundamental issues, such as mechanical design, sensor calibration, power management, and time synchronization, arise regardless of sensor types. We reflect on these challenges and share key insights to guide future platform designs, enhancing reproducibility and robustness in autonomous vehicle testing. We also summarize the mitigation strategies we adopted, for instance, system-wide time synchronization using GNSS timing and NTP protocols, custom calibration routines for different sensor configurations, and design practices to improve reliability and data integrity during field deployments.

</details>


### [75] [Emergency Obstacle Avoidance Maneuvers in Differential-Drive Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05607)
*Kautik Mandve,Nilay Kant*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过540次分块随机试验，研究差速驱动机器人在不同接近速度下紧急避障的机动性能与里程计误差，发现反向旋转机动的编码器偏差随速度增大，且与避障成功率下降相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急避障要求机器人在可用距离内完成制动或转向，而机动性能与里程计误差对接近速度的依赖关系尚不明确，因此需要速度相关的实证刻画。

Method: 采用足迹间隙分析与归一化轮运动指标对五种制动与转向机动进行运动学描述；在瓷砖地面上开展540次分块随机试验（保留539次），指令接近速度为45、55、65 cm/s，并用顶置相机提供独立位姿参考。

Result: 在完整运动记录上，两种反向旋转机动从最低到最高速度的编码器平均偏差增加4.7–5.1度，而圆弧与制动辅助枢转变化小于0.4度；较大编码器偏差在控制距离、速度、机动、日期与转向方向后与更低的避障成功率相关；陀螺偏差在反向旋转中约保持3度；并给出90%成功率下的估计反应距离及其不确定性。

Conclusion: 结果支持对紧急机动进行速度特定的经验刻画，并将编码器误差与恢复运动及测量窗口不匹配区分开来。

Abstract: Emergency obstacle avoidance requires a mobile robot to brake or change its heading within the available distance. This paper investigates the dependence of maneuver performance and odometric error on approach speed for a differential-drive robot. A footprint-clearance analysis and a normalized wheel-motion index provide a kinematic description of five braking and turning maneuvers. The principal experiment comprises 540 block-randomized trials on tile, of which 539 are retained, at commanded approach speeds of 45, 55, and 65 cm/s. An overhead camera provides an independent pose reference. When encoder, gyro, and camera heading changes are evaluated over complete motion records, the mean encoder discrepancy increases by 4.7-5.1 degrees for the two reverse-spin maneuvers between the lowest and highest speeds; the arc and brake-assisted pivot change by less than 0.4 degrees. Larger encoder discrepancy is associated with lower avoidance success after adjustment for distance, speed, maneuver, day, and turn direction. Gyro discrepancies remain approximately 3 degrees for the reverse spins. Estimated reaction distances for 90% success and their uncertainty quantify the maneuver trade-offs. The results support speed-specific empirical characterization of emergency maneuvers and distinguish encoder error from recovery motion and measurement-window mismatch.

</details>


### [76] [FOCUS: Fine-Grained Open-Vocabulary Change Detection for Uncertainty-Aware Semi-Static Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05639)
*Can Xu,Mingfeng Yuan,Mahan Mohammadi,Steven L. Waslander*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FOCUS 框架，通过融合几何与 3D 高斯渲染外观的概率推断，实现半静态环境下的物体级变化检测与地图维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的自主机器人需在多次访问间保持环境记忆的更新。在半静态环境中，物体可能被几何与语义上相似的实例原地替换，仅凭几何或粗略语义难以检测这种身份变化，因此需要一个新的变化检测与地图维护方法。

Method: 将半静态记忆维护建模为概率推断，融合几何似然与由 3D 高斯地图渲染的外观似然；采用递归三状态估计器判断每个已映射物体处于 PERSISTED、REPLACED 或 REMOVED。为应对 3DGS 渲染的不完美，将渲染外观证据建模为概率而非直接变化分数，参数由初始建图会话的无变化重放自动校准。

Result: 在带有模糊原地替换的新 Isaac Sim 仓库基准和真实世界 TorWIC 数据集上评估，物体级替换 F1 从概率基线的 0.20 提升至 0.84，并可无需人工参数调整迁移到真实数据。

Conclusion: FOCUS 通过不确定性感知的概率融合，有效解决半静态环境中相似实例原地替换的检测难题，且具备良好的真实世界可迁移性。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating over long periods must keep their environmental memory up to date as the world changes between visits. In semi-static environments, an object may be replaced in place by a different but geometrically and semantically similar instance, making the identity change difficult to detect from geometry or coarse semantics alone. We present FOCUS, an uncertainty-aware framework for object-level change detection and map maintenance. We formulate semi-static memory maintenance as probabilistic inference that fuses geometric likelihood with appearance likelihood rendered from a 3D Gaussian map. A recursive three-state estimator maintains whether each mapped object is PERSISTED, REPLACED, or REMOVED. To account for imperfect 3DGS rendering, we model the rendered appearance evidence probabilistically rather than using it as a direct change score, with the model parameters automatically calibrated from a change-free replay of the initial mapping session. We evaluate our method on a new Isaac Sim warehouse benchmark with ambiguous in-place replacements and on the real-world TorWIC dataset. It improves object-level replacement F1 from 0.20 to 0.84 over a probabilistic baseline and transfers to real-world data without manual parameter retuning.

</details>


### [77] [Spacecraft Rendezvous Trajectory Generation with Modular Constraints via Diffusion Model Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05642)
*Mariko A. Storey-Matsutani,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散模型的轨迹生成方法，用于交会与近距离操作（RPO），通过组合不同能量模型实现灵活的任务约束配置，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨服务、卫星检查、主动碎片清除等新兴任务需要能适应多种场景的轨迹设计方法，传统方法难以灵活配置任务约束。

Method: 先分别训练基于能量的扩散模型以满足不同约束（如接近锥、传感器视线），推理时将各能量模型相互组合或与解析定义的能量场组合，以施加特定约束组合。

Result: 组合学习的接近锥模型与传感器视线模型、接近锥模型与合成避障模型，两者的约束满足率均与单约束模型相差不超过1个百分点或更高。

Conclusion: 该组合式扩散框架为RPO轨迹设计提供了模块化方法，可在无需重新训练的情况下支持新约束组合的重配置。

Abstract: Emerging mission classes such as on-orbit servicing, satellite inspection, and active debris removal require trajectory design methods that are adaptable to a variety of mission scenarios. We present a diffusion-based trajectory generation approach for rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) that enables flexible configuration of mission constraints. First, individual energy-based diffusion models are trained to satisfy distinct constraints such as approach cone and sensor line-of-sight from a set of optimized trajectories. Then, at inference time, the learned energy models can be composed with one another, or with an analytically defined energy field, to enforce specific constraint combinations. We validate this framework with the composition of a learned approach cone model and a learned sensor line-of-sight model, as well as a learned approach cone model and synthetic obstacle avoidance model, both of which yield constraint satisfaction rates that are within 1 percentage point of the single-constraint models or higher. These results indicate that our compositional diffusion framework can provide a modular approach to RPO trajectory design and enable reconfiguration for new constraint combinations without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [78] [Bayesian Data Augmentation for DNN Retraining with Binomial Outcomes in Vision-Based UAV Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05674)
*Ashik E Rasul,Hyung-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过贝叶斯数据增强与高保真仿真器迭代重训练停机坪检测DNN，以着陆性能为目标优化，提升着陆成功率与置信度稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止或杂乱城市环境中，视觉着陆对无人机任务可靠性至关重要；真实着陆点多非结构化且多变，要求感知系统具备强泛化能力。合成数据增强训练DNN虽具可扩展性，但DNN重训练计算代价高、试飞验证困难，限制了穷举式微调，因此需要优化的重训练流程。

Method: 部署贝叶斯数据增强框架，集成具备高保真飞行动力学的逼真仿真器，迭代重训练停机坪检测DNN，并以着陆性能作为目标函数进行优化。

Result: 在逼真仿真器不同环境条件与飞行器状态下的实验表明，着陆性能得到提升，预测着陆结果的置信区间更窄。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效优化停机坪检测模型的迭代重训练，提升视觉着陆性能与预测稳定性。

Abstract: In GPS-denied or cluttered urban environments, vision-based landing is essential for reliable UAV missions. Real-world landing sites are often unstructured and highly variable, requiring strong generalization by the perception system. Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) trained with synthetic data augmentation offer a scalable solution for learning landing-site features across diverse vehicle and environmental states. However, computationally expensive DNN retraining, along with challenging performance validation via test flights, limits exhaustive model fine-tuning and necessitates an optimized retraining pipeline. In this work, we deploy a Bayesian data augmentation framework integrated with a photorealistic simulator featuring high-fidelity vehicle dynamics to iteratively retrain the helipad detector DNN, maximizing landing performance as the objective function.
  We validate our framework with experiments in a photorealistic simulator under different environmental conditions and vehicle states, demonstrating improved landing performance and tighter confidence intervals on predicted landing outcomes.

</details>


### [79] [Learning Coordinated Visuomotor Box-Pushing from Solo Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05677)
*Ryusei Matsumoto,Mei Kinjo,Yoko Sasaki,Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究无通信、机载分散式多机器人模仿学习，以大规模协同推箱为测试场景，探索能否利用单机器人遥操作数据训练多机器人协同策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人模仿学习中，集体演示数据难以采集，因为单个操作者无法同时控制多台机器人；而许多协同任务主要靠减少机器人间干扰来实现系统级效率，这为利用单机器人数据提供了可能。

Method: 系统研究数据集构建策略与轻量级策略架构，在无通信、机载分散部署条件下，以协同推箱任务为测试平台，最多使用40台机器人进行实验。

Result: 实验表明，仅通过被动观察其他运行中的机器人难以获得有效协同，揭示了多机器人研究中的一个具体瓶颈。

Conclusion: 利用单机器人遥操作数据实现大规模多机器人协同仍面临挑战，被动观察不足以支撑有效协调，数据集构建与策略设计是关键难点。

Abstract: Multi-robot imitation learning, particularly in settings where visuomotor policies are deployed in a communication-free, onboard decentralised style, represents an attractive paradigm. However, its realisation remains insufficiently understood, largely due to the difficulty of collecting collective demonstrations, since a single operator cannot control many robots simultaneously. Meanwhile, unlike coupled collaborative manipulation, many coordinated tasks achieve system-wide efficiency primarily through minimising inter-robot interference. This structure motivates us to study whether data collected by a teleoperated single-robot can be leveraged for large-scale coordinated box-pushing as a testbed. We systematically investigate dataset creation strategies and lightweight policy architectures. In particular, experiments with up to 40 robots highlight the difficulty of acquiring effective coordination solely through passive observation of other operating robots, revealing a concrete bottleneck for multi-robot research.

</details>


### [80] [Dataset-Free Compliant Humanoid Loco-Manipulation with Dynamic Online Posture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05678)
*Seungho Yeom,Zhenyu Wu,Jaeyoung Huh,Diego Williams,Yuheng Zhi,Soofiyan Atar,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCLO是一种无需人体运动数据、仅通过两个末端执行器目标指令的人形机器人全身移动操作系统，利用解析可达性先验和策略闭环采样在线生成骨盆高度与躯干姿态，并学习全身柔顺性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人移动操作控制器大多依赖人体运动数据来学习全身协调与姿态，导致策略依赖外部数据源提供此类信息，限制了其自主性和泛化能力。

Method: OCLO仅通过两个末端执行器目标进行指令控制；由于目标不能唯一确定全身姿态，系统使用解析可达性先验在线生成骨盆高度与躯干朝向，并通过策略闭环采样、以任务无关的代价函数进一步细化；同时通过弹簧-阻尼模型依据测量力位移末端执行器参考，学习全身柔顺，使腿、腰和骨盆对外部载荷产生顺应。

Result: 仿真中，使用可达性先验使获取指令参考的成功率达77.8%，远高于无先验时的37.8%；细化步骤降低了所有评估任务中的末端执行器朝向误差。同一姿态模块在五项任务中的四项上提升了预训练SONIC控制器的性能。缺乏柔顺训练时，策略在扰动下更易失去平衡而非牺牲跟踪。在Unitree G1上，OCLO能在导致消融实验失败的末端执行器扰动下保持平衡，并完成七项移动操作任务，包括蹲姿行走和从低矮表面拾取箱子。

Conclusion: OCLO证明了无需人体运动数据、仅凭两个末端执行器目标即可实现人形机器人全身移动操作，解析可达性先验、在线细化与柔顺训练对提升成功率和抗扰动能力至关重要，并在真实机器人上验证了多种复杂任务。

Abstract: Most humanoid loco-manipulation controllers require human motion data to learn whole-body coordination and posture, leaving policies reliant on external sources to provide this data. We present OCLO (Online-posture Compliant LOco-manipulation), a humanoid loco-manipulation system trained without human motion data and commanded only through two end-effector targets. Because these targets do not uniquely determine whole-body posture, OCLO generates pelvis height and torso orientation online using an analytic reachability prior, further refined through policy-in-the-loop sampling with a task-agnostic cost. OCLO also learns whole-body compliance by displacing end-effector references according to measured forces through a spring-damper model, encouraging the legs, waist, and pelvis to yield to external loads. In simulation, using the reachability prior leads to a 77.8% success rate in acquiring the commanded reference, a vast improvement over the 37.8% success rate accomplished without the prior. Further, refinement reduces end-effector orientation error across all evaluated tasks. The same posture module improves a pretrained SONIC controller on four of five tasks. Without compliance training, policies tend to lose balance under disturbances rather than sacrifice tracking. On a Unitree G1, OCLO maintains balance under end-effector disturbances that cause its ablations to fail and performs seven loco-manipulation tasks, including crouched walking and picking up a box from a low surface. Project website: https://oclo-humanoid.github.io/

</details>


### [81] [Benchmarking Generative Trajectory Models for Active-Inference Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05692)
*Yulin Li,Mohsen A. Jafari,Andrea Matta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出生成式主动推理控制（GenAIF），用单一生成轨迹模型同时提供目标条件策略分布与状态到观测的似然映射，并在MuJoCo操作任务中比较扩散模型、自回归Transformer、CVAE和流匹配的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统动力学难以显式建模，而从轨迹示范中学习为主动推理控制提供了可行路径；论文希望统一动作提议、受控预测与观测证据三个环节。

Method: 提出GenAIF框架，令一个生成轨迹模型从示范和测得的动作干预中学习，进而导出三项模型要求：有用的动作提议、施加动作下的准确预测、以及用于信念更新和期望信息增益的概率观测证据；在多种物理条件下的MuJoCo操作任务中基准测试扩散、自回归Transformer、CVAE与流匹配。

Result: 扩散模型在所测动力学下取得最强控制效果；CVAE在短时程预测上表现相当且推理速度更快；正确的条件设置起决定性作用；轨迹复用可进一步节省计算。使用同一冻结模型的隐藏动力学实验中，倾斜突变后出现即时信念适应，但随后的不稳定表明持续推理仍是挑战。

Conclusion: 研究支持在GenAIF中采用共享生成轨迹模型，将动作提议、受控预测与观测证据统一连接；扩散与CVAE各有优势，持续推理的稳定性是未来待解决问题。

Abstract: Learning from trajectory demonstrations offers a route to active-inference control of complex systems whose dynamics are difficult to model explicitly. We introduce generative active-inference control (GenAIF), in which one generative trajectory model learns from demonstrations and measured action interventions to supply a goal-conditioned policy distribution and a state-to-observation likelihood mapping. From this control design, we derive three model requirements: (i) useful action proposals, (ii) accurate prediction under imposed actions, and (iii) probabilistic observation evidence for belief updating and expected information gain. We benchmark diffusion, autoregressive Transformers, conditional variational autoencoders (CVAEs), and flow matching in a MuJoCo manipulation task with multiple physical conditions. Diffusion delivers the strongest control across the tested dynamics, while CVAE combines comparable short-horizon prediction with much faster inference. Correct conditioning is decisive, and trajectory reuse offers further computational savings. With the same frozen models, a hidden-dynamics experiment demonstrates prompt belief adaptation after an unannounced tilt change; subsequent instability identifies sustained inference as a remaining challenge. These findings support the use of shared generative trajectory models to connect action proposal, controlled prediction, and observation evidence within GenAIF.

</details>


### [82] [Virtual model control for compliant reaching under uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05695)
*Yi Zhang,Daniel Larby,Fumiya Iida,Fulvio Forni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究将虚拟模型控制（VMC）框架从腿部机器人运动扩展到力控机械臂的到达任务，提出六种虚拟模型设计方法，并在8自由度人形机器人上通过三类实验验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VMC 以往主要用于腿式机器人的运动控制，但其在复杂不确定环境中设计力控机器人控制器的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。作者希望探究 VMC 是否能更普遍地适用于其他类型机器人系统，特别是力控机械臂的到达任务。

Method: 提出六种针对到达任务的虚拟模型设计方法，用于在含障碍和不确定性的环境中完成到达。使用一台力控8自由度人形机器人在真实世界中验证。设计三类实验，分别考察 VMC 控制器的可预测性、对外部力的敏感性，以及对已知和未知障碍的适应性。

Result: 实验分析表明，所提方法虽然需要牺牲一定的精度和轨迹最优性，但能够在不确定性条件下以直观且可扩展的方式设计复杂的到达运动。

Conclusion: VMC 框架可推广至力控机械臂的到达任务，在精度与最优性上有所让步，但换来了在不确定环境中的直观性与可扩展性，为复杂运动设计提供了一种有效途径。

Abstract: Virtual Model Control (VMC) is an approach to design a controller for force-controlled robots in complex uncertain environments. While this method was primarily investigated for legged robot locomotion in the past, it can be more generally applicable to other types of robotic systems. This paper investigates the VMC framework for reaching tasks in a force-controlled robotic arm. We propose six different approaches to designing virtual models in order to achieve reaching tasks in environments with obstacles and uncertainties. A force-controlled 8 degree-of-freedom humanoid robot was used to validate the proposed approach in the real world. We conducted three experiments to test the performance of VMC controllers in terms of predictability, sensitivity to external force, and adaptability against known and unknown obstacles. Experimental analyses show that, even though the proposed approach needs to sacrifice accuracy and trajectory optimality, it enables us to design complex reaching motions under uncertainties, in an intuitive and extendable manner.

</details>


### [83] [When to Switch: Reliable Action-Chunk Extension for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05719)
*Seonghoon Yu,Dongwon Kim,HyungRok Jung,Yoonjae Baek,Byung-kwan Lee,Suha Kwak,Jeany Son*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RACE 框架，通过预测并条件化子技能切换时机，实现长动作块（action chunk）的可靠执行，减少 VLA 模型推理停顿，在仿真与真机上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型推理开销大，机器人在策略调用间需暂停，形成停顿式（stop-and-go）执行，破坏动作平滑性并延长任务时间。延长动作块可减少调用次数，但预测更长未来导致长块执行不可靠，作者据此分析误差来源。

Method: 分析长动作块内的动作误差，发现误差集中在操作子技能切换处并随块长急剧增长，由此强调“切换时机”的重要性。提出 RACE（Reliable Action-Chunk Extension），用一次辅助的一步去噪过程预测切换时机，并将动作生成条件化在该时机上，从而降低子技能切换处的误差，支持更长动作块的可靠执行。

Result: 仿真基准中，RACE 在相同块长下优于微调；使用 2 倍长块时成功率超过近期 SOTA 与高效 VLA，4 倍长块时仍具竞争力。真机上使用 4 倍长块，将停顿式执行的空闲时间减少约 5 倍，成功率高于同块长的微调方法。

Conclusion: 子技能切换时机是长动作块不可靠的关键因素，显式学习并条件化该时机可显著提升长块执行的可靠性，同时大幅降低推理停顿带来的空闲时间，验证了方法的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models serve as unified policies for robotic manipulation, yet their expensive inference forces robots to pause between policy calls, resulting in stop-and-go execution that interrupts smooth motion and prolongs task completion. Extending the action chunk reduces policy calls and hence these pauses, but predicting farther into the future makes long-chunk execution unreliable. To understand where this unreliability arises, we analyze action errors within long chunks and find that they concentrate around transitions between manipulation subskills, growing sharply with chunk length. This suggests the importance of transition timing, i.e., when to switch subskills within a chunk. Motivated by this observation, we introduce RACE (Reliable Action-Chunk Extension), a framework that predicts the transition timing from an auxiliary one-step denoising pass and conditions action generation on it. By learning and conditioning on transition timing, RACE reduces errors at subskill transitions and enables reliable execution of longer action chunks. Across simulation benchmarks, RACE outperforms fine-tuning at the same chunk length; with 2x longer chunks, it surpasses recent state-of-the-art and efficient VLAs in success rate, and with 4x longer chunks, it remains competitive. On a real robot, RACE uses 4x longer chunks, which reduces the idle time caused by stop-and-go execution by about 5x, while achieving a higher success rate than fine-tuning with the same chunk length. Code and a real-robot demo are available at https://github.com/Seonghoon-Yu/RACE-VLA

</details>


### [84] [End-to-End Safe Social Navigation via Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning and Probabilistic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05733)
*Tommaso Van Der Meer Andrea Garulli,Antonio Giannitrapani,Renato Quartullo,Alberto Vaglio,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JESSI：基于JAX的端到端安全社交可解释导航框架，直接从原始LiDAR扫描映射到运动可行的控制命令，通过Dirichlet参数化连续动作空间与确定性边界提升安全性，并集成注意力感知模块提取概率化人类状态，实现可解释的社交感知决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主社交导航需平衡效率、物理安全与社交合规。现有强化学习方法虽有效，但常依赖不现实的假设，例如已知人类的位置和速度。因此需要一种不依赖此类先验假设的轻量级端到端方案。

Method: 提出JESSI框架：轻量级端到端强化学习，将原始LiDAR扫描直接映射为运动可行的控制命令；采用Dirichlet参数化的连续动作空间与确定性边界增强安全性；集成注意力感知模块，提取概率化人类状态以支持可解释、社交感知的决策；并以多任务范式将RL策略与监督感知信号联合优化。

Result: 通过大量仿真实验及在差速驱动机器人上的真实部署验证：与RL策略联合优化的监督感知信号在多任务范式下能增强社交行为；JESSI在导航成功率与社交行为方面均优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: JESSI证明无需依赖人类位置与速度等先验假设，即可在端到端RL框架中同时实现高导航成功率与优越社交行为，兼顾效率、安全与社交合规。

Abstract: Autonomous social navigation requires balancing efficiency, physical safety, and social compliance. Reinforcement Learning (RL) methods provide a viable and effective solution but often rely on unrealistic assumptions, such as the knowledge of humans' position and velocity. In this paper, we introduce JESSI (JAX-based E2E Safe Social Interpretable navigation), a lightweight end-to-end RL framework that maps raw LiDAR scans directly to kinematically feasible control commands. JESSI enhances safety via Dirichlet-parameterized continuous action spaces and deterministic bounding, while an integrated attention-based perception module extracts probabilistic human states for interpretable, socially aware decision-making. Through extensive simulations and real-world deployment on a differential-drive robot, we demonstrate that jointly optimizing the RL policy with a supervised perception signal in a multi-task paradigm enhances social behavior. Ultimately, JESSI is able to balance high navigation success rates and superior social behaviors compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [85] [FreeSpeed: Training-Free Speed Control for Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05734)
*Yuxuan Hu,Shilin Shan,Qiheng Wang,Jinghan Yang,Junqiao Fan,Hao Wan,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练模块FreeSpeed，通过方向不一致性自适应调整动作块执行速度，实现在线调速且不损失成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人部署需要在线控制执行速度（加速应对时间约束、减速保障安全与人机交互），但模仿学习策略继承示范速度，测试时改速会引入不可恢复的分布外观测，降低任务成功率。

Method: 观察到动作块的方向不一致性可反映任务阶段关键性，据此设计FreeSpeed：对预训练策略的动作块进行后处理，按请求速率重采样，并以相邻动作间的方向不一致性作为主要信号进行重缩放，自适应决定执行速度可接近请求速度的程度。

Result: 在3类策略家族与50个仿真任务中支持在线调速，保持单任务成功前提下实现0.22x–2.53x执行速率；在4个真实操作任务中平均成功率94.0%（冻结策略93.8%），执行速率0.38x–1.97x。

Conclusion: FreeSpeed为免训练即插即用模块，能在不损害任务成功的前提下实现灵活的在线速度调节，兼顾仿真与真实场景。

Abstract: Online control of execution speed is essential for deploying robot policies in real-world scenarios, as robots may need to speed up under time constraints or slow down to facilitate human interaction and improve safety. However, imitation-learned policies inherit the execution speed of their demonstrations, and test-time speed modification can introduce unrecoverable out-of-distribution observations, reducing task success. We observe that the directional inconsistency of action chunks reflects task-phase criticality, indicating how aggressively action step lengths can be modified while preserving task success. Based on this observation, we introduce FreeSpeed, a training-free module that post-processes action chunks from pretrained policies. FreeSpeed resamples each predicted chunk at the requested rate, then uses directional inconsistency between adjacent actions as the primary signal for rescaling. This signal adaptively determines how closely the execution speed can approach the requested speed, allowing flexible speed adjustment within the evaluated limits without compromising task success. Across three policy families and 50 simulated tasks, FreeSpeed supports online speed changes, with realized execution rates spanning 0.22x to 2.53x among settings that preserve per-task success. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, FreeSpeed achieves an average success rate of 94.0%, matching the frozen policy's 93.8%, while realizing execution rates from 0.38x to 1.97x.

</details>


### [86] [The Unexpired Plan: A Free Monitor for Accelerated Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05747)
*Yi Zhao,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向扩散策略训练-free加速的"监视器"框架，将监视器视为"统计量"与"触发后响应"两部分的组合设计，并以闭环成功率而非特征距离来衡量监视器的价值。实验覆盖114种加速器配置与四个策略家族，证明遗忘式响应（absorbing）配以低频触发、度量"相对被替代策略的偏离"的统计量最为经济，可将每次调用计算量降至1.55–3.09倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练-free的扩散策略加速通常依赖内部相似度信号来判断"捷径是否改变结果"，但既有方法仅以特征距离为标准，忽略了监视器在闭环任务中的真实代价。作者希望以闭环成功率为统一度量，重新审视监视器设计（读什么统计量、触发后如何处理），并回答罕见触发、度量偏离的统计量能否让"永不遗忘"的响应变得可负担。

Method: 将监视器拆解为两个独立设计维度：(1)所读取的统计量；(2)统计量触发后的响应方式（重新武装re-arm vs 吸收absorbing）。提出以"相对被替代策略的偏离"作为统计量，利用chunked策略尚未过期的上一份计划作为免费参考信号。在114种加速器配置、四个策略家族以及一个接触丰富的第五家族上进行闭环评估，并与oracle、无监控基线等对照。

Result: 两个加速器各自通过自身门槛并将每次复用记为"已认证"，但同一任务下一个能完成所有回合、另一个一个都完成不了。已发表的门控在每次拒绝后重新武装，即使给oracle全部前向传播与精确局部动作误差也会损失20个点；改用吸收响应仅损失1个点，并保留大部分速度。以低频触发统计量度量相对被替代策略的偏离，可将每次调用计算量降低1.55–3.09倍；同样覆盖下任何需要参考的检查将不得不完全停止加速。四个家族中有三个仅调度即可保持预设±2点裕度；监视器的价值体现在第四家族（挽救候选选择）、四个未被选中的配置，以及跑在所有设计冻结之后的接触丰富第五家族——该家族中无监控臂在任何速度下都守不住裕度，而监控臂可以。

Conclusion: 监视器不是单一设计，而是"统计量+响应"的联合设计。以闭环成功率定价监视器，可证明"永不遗忘"的absorbing响应之所以可负担，关键在于统计量罕见触发且度量的是相对被替代策略的偏离——这一参考信号对chunked策略而言已经付过代价、可免费读取。该框架在多个策略家族与配置上验证了其加速度与鲁棒性优势。

Abstract: Training-free acceleration of a diffusion policy is accepted when an internal similarity signal reports that the shortcut changed nothing. We price every monitor a control loop can afford in closed-loop success rather than feature distance, over $114$ accelerator configurations and four policy families: two accelerators each clear their own gate's bar and log every reuse as certified, while on one task one finishes every episode and the other none. A monitor is two designs, not one --- the statistic it reads, and what it does when that statistic fires. Published gates re-arm after every rejection, and under that response even an oracle handed every forward pass and the exact local action error loses twenty points; absorbing the same statistic costs one point, and most of its speed. What makes a response that never forgets affordable is a statistic that rarely fires, and what it must measure is deviation from the policy the accelerator replaced --- which a chunked policy has already paid for, its last plan not yet expired and free to read. Guarding every call this way cuts per-call compute by $1.55$--$3.09\times$, where any reference-requiring check at the same coverage would have to stop accelerating altogether. On three of our four families the schedule alone already holds the pre-stated $\pm2$-point margin. What the monitor is measurably worth shows in three places: on the fourth family, where it rescues the candidate selection landed on; on four configurations it did not select; and on a contact-rich fifth family, chosen where the schedule was expected to fail and run after every design choice was frozen, where no unmonitored arm at its speed holds the margin and the monitored one does.

</details>


### [87] [Demonstration-Calibrated Port-Hamiltonian Retuning for Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05755)
*Yulong Yang,Fan Wu,Christine Allen-Blanchette,Amit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PHRetune，一种无需评估回放或增益搜索的离线方法，从示教中学习端口哈密顿模型，为冻结策略导出下游阻抗控制器的增益缩放，提升扩散与VLA策略的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略与VLA策略在操作任务中常部署于下游阻抗控制器，其刚度与阻尼增益影响任务成功率，但通常沿用数据采集时的设置而非针对部署策略选择。经验性增益扫描虽可提升性能，却需要大量评估回放。

Method: 从示教数据学习端口哈密顿模型，估计策略预测动作对应的努力与能量；将策略应用于记录的示教观测，把预测结果与示教导出的努力、能量预算进行对比；据此以闭式解导出单一增益缩放系数，调节下游控制器，同时保持策略及其动作表示不变。增益在评估前固定，无需先验机械臂模型、任务奖励、策略重训练或额外运行时计算。

Result: 在LIBERO套件上，PHRetune将扩散策略成功率最高提升9.4个百分点，导出的增益在经验增益扫描中取得最高成功率。在四项真实世界操作任务中，PHRetuned扩散策略优于标称策略、其他增益调优方法及策略重训练基线。同一流程在SmolVLA与OpenVLA-OFT上于所有任务均提升成功率，同时降低两种VLA主干的加速度与抖动。

Conclusion: PHRetune提供了一种离线、无需回放与搜索的增益导出方法，可普适地提升扩散与VLA策略在阻抗控制器下的操作性能，并改善运动平滑性。

Abstract: Diffusion and VLA policies for manipulation are often deployed through downstream impedance controllers. The stiffness and damping gains of these controllers affect task success, yet are commonly inherited from data collection rather than selected for the deployed policy. Although empirical gain sweeps can improve performance, they require repeated evaluation rollouts. We introduce PHRetune, an offline method that derives controller gains for a frozen policy without evaluation rollouts or gain search. Our approach learns a port-Hamiltonian model from demonstrations to estimate the effort and energy associated with the policy's predicted actions. The policy is applied to recorded demonstration observations, and its predictions are assessed against demonstration-derived effort and energy budgets. From this comparison, we derive a single gain scale in closed form, adjusting the downstream controller while preserving the policy and its action representation. The gains are fixed before evaluation, without requiring a prior manipulator model, task rewards, policy retraining, or additional runtime computation. Across LIBERO suites, PHRetune improves Diffusion Policy success by up to 9.4 percentage points, with the derived gains achieving the highest observed success rates in empirical gain sweeps. On all four real-world manipulation tasks, PHRetuned Diffusion Policy outperforms the nominal policy, alternative gain-tuning methods, and a policy-retraining baseline. The same procedure improves success with SmolVLA and OpenVLA-OFT on every task, while reducing acceleration and jerk for both VLA backbones.

</details>


### [88] [Human-in-the-Loop Neuro-Symbolic Drift Anticipation for Reliable Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05757)
*Junhyun Nam,Wonse Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出混合式HDS框架，用于视觉SLAM漂移预判，借助LLM作为推理桥梁，将人类上下文知识转化为符号约束，结合神经网络风险估计，提升可靠性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的漂移预测模型具备预测能力，但黑箱特性使其在分布外（OOD）环境中常产生物理上不一致的输出。

Method: 提出Human-in-the-Loop的神经符号框架Hybrid DeepSEE（HDS）：融合神经漂移风险估计与符号约束推理，并用大语言模型作为推理桥梁，把定性的人类上下文转化为可解释的符号约束。

Result: 在既有架构基础上提出了一种更优的漂移预判框架，在视觉SLAM中实现了更强的可靠性与一致性。

Conclusion: 神经符号结合与人在回路的推理有望克服纯数据驱动方法的物理不一致问题，为视觉SLAM的漂移预判提供更可靠、可解释的方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces Hybrid DeepSEE (HDS), a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) neuro-symbolic framework for proactive drift anticipation in Visual SLAM (V-SLAM). While data-driven models offer predictive power, their "black-box" nature often yields physically inconsistent outputs in out-of-distribution (OOD) environments. To address this, HDS integrates neural drift risk estimation with symbolic constraint reasoning. By utilizing a Large Language Model (LLM) as a reasoning bridge, the framework translates qualitative human context into interpretable symbolic constraints. Building upon this architecture, we propose a superior drift anticipation framework that ensures enhanced reliability and consistency in Visual SLAM

</details>


### [89] [Bilinear Flow Policy: Distributional Extrapolation for Goal-Conditioned Visuomotor Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05765)
*Wonsuhk Jung,Sundhar Vinodh Sangeetha,Chen Xu,Abhishek Gupta,Masha Itkina,Shreyas Kousik,Haruki Nishimura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Bilinear Flow Policy (BFP)，通过转导式检索与双线性条件流，将未见观测-目标对重构为锚点加残差，实现多模态行为下的分布外目标外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标条件模仿学习（GCIL）结合流匹配可表示多模态行为并适应多样目标，但当目标超出示范支持域时往往失效。作者将"在保持多模态不塌缩的前提下外推到未见目标"定义为分布外推问题。

Method: BFP结合转导式检索与双线性条件流：对未见观测-目标对，检索一个"锚点"训练样本，并将其重构为锚点加残差项；残差需紧凑编码当前对与锚点的差异，锚点需使该差异对动作分布具有预测性。方法使用预训练视觉特征与一种新学习的锚点选择算法，双线性流建模锚点与残差如何共同决定多模态动作分布。

Result: 在五个仿真操作任务中，BFP的分布外成功率是GCIL策略的2.63倍，是最强外推基线方法的1.36倍；在两项真实任务中相较GCIL提升32%。

Conclusion: 理论上证明：在合适假设下，双线性流在未见目标处的动作分布误差可由分布内流匹配误差界定（相差问题相关因子）；该理论还给出预测哪些策略能外推良好、外推到哪些目标的实际部署前诊断方法。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned imitation learning (GCIL) with flow matching is a promising framework that can represent multimodal behaviors while adapting to diverse, user-specified goals, yet often fails when goals lie outside the demonstration support. To extrapolate to such unseen goals without collapsing multimodality - a problem we call distributional extrapolation - we introduce Bilinear Flow Policy (BFP), a generative visuomotor policy that combines transductive retrieval with a bilinear conditional flow. Given an unseen observation-goal pair, BFP retrieves an "anchor" training example and transductively reformulates the unseen pair as this familiar anchor plus a residual term. For this decomposition to guide action prediction, the residual must compactly encode how the current observation-goal pair differs from the anchor, and the anchor must be chosen so that this difference is predictive of the corresponding action distribution. BFP achieves this with pretrained visual features and a novel learned anchor-selection algorithm. The novel bilinear flow then models how the anchor and the residual jointly determine the multimodal action distribution. We prove that, for bilinear flow under suitable assumptions, action distribution error at unseen goals is bounded by the in-distribution flow-matching error up to problem-dependent factors. Across five manipulation tasks in simulation, BFP achieves 2.63x the out-of distribution success rate of a GCIL policy and 1.36x that of the strongest extrapolation-targeted baseline. On two real-world tasks, BFP improves over GCIL by 32%. Finally, our theory yields practical, pre deployment diagnostics for predicting which trained policies will extrapolate well and to which unseen goal.

</details>


### [90] [DASH: A da Vinci Adapter for Serial-link and Humanoid Robots as an Accessible Platform for Surgical Robotics Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05792)
*Sara Wickenhiser,Junrong Zhou,Zekai Liang,Lizzie Peiros,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出DASH，通过两种适配器将da Vinci手术器械无需改装地安装到通用机器人上，实现外科机器人的低成本遥操作研究平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专用手术机器人平台成本高、基础设施要求高，限制了农村和资源匮乏机构的可及性；此前工作虽已用通用机器人遥操作完成腹腔镜与活体手术任务，但依赖手持器械与被动连杆耦合，而非机器人本体的原生驱动。

Method: 提出DASH（da Vinci Adapter for Serial-link and Humanoid platforms），包含两种适配器，无需修改器械本体；适配器兼容多种机器人负载能力，可触发器械原生锁扣，并无线识别插入的器械以加载对应的运动学与耦合矩阵。

Result: 遥操作实验验证了DASH的有效性，并展示了其在为外科机器人平台提供研究访问途径方面的优势。

Conclusion: DASH使通用机器人能够以原生驱动方式集成da Vinci手术器械，可融入临床工作流程，为资源受限场景和研究用途提供了低成本的外科机器人研究平台方案。

Abstract: Robotic minimally invasive surgery offers well-documented clinical benefits, but the cost and infrastructure requirements of purpose-built platforms limit access in rural and lower-resourced facilities. Recent work has teleoperated general-purpose robots for laparoscopic tasks and in vivo procedures, but relied on handheld instruments coupled through passive linkages rather than native robotic actuation. Instead, we adapt da Vinci Classic and Xi instruments onto general-purpose robots that can integrate in clinical workflows. We present DASH, a da Vinci Adapter for Serial-link and Humanoid platforms, consisting of two types of adapters that require no modification to the instruments themselves. Each adapter is compatible with many robotic platform load capacities, engages the instrument's native latch, and wirelessly identifies inserted tools to load instrument-specific kinematics and coupling matrices. Teleoperated experiments demonstrate the efficacy of DASH and its benefits for enabling research access to surgical robotic platforms.

</details>


### [91] [Dynamics-Aware Adaptive Corridors with Feasibility-Perturbed Trust-Region SQP for Certified Nonholonomic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05796)
*Yang Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于带倒挡的类车车辆的泊车规划器，使优化阶段每次迭代均严格满足离散动力学且车辆矩形在各采样点间不碰障碍，并在基准测试中取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于优化的泊车规划器仅在采样时刻施加碰撞约束，车辆角点可能在采样点间切入障碍；且在求解器收敛前不存在可执行轨迹。本文旨在让优化过程的每次迭代都可行、无碰撞且动力学精确满足。

Method: 为每个时间区间构造一个凸走廊，包含两端所有车辆角点，并按扫掠余量收缩以限制角点路径偏离弦的距离；初始猜测发生碰撞时用分离半平面赋予走廊离开障碍的方向；随后根据当前迭代的速度、曲率和步长生成朝向对齐的盒子并在接受步后重建。采用可行性扰动的信赖域序列二次规划，通过反馈将每步投影到动力学上并精确验证。

Result: 在820个基准案例中成功818例且无穿透（797例从初始猜测即成功），7103个优化阶段迭代无一不可用；当99%的初始猜测与障碍相交时成功率仍达96.5%；机动时间中位数比同样认证的精确碰撞基线仅长0.7%。

Conclusion: 该方法保证每次迭代精确满足离散动力学并保持车辆矩形在采样点间无碰撞，代价单调下降，走廊稳定后极限点为走廊约束问题的KKT点或违反约束资格。但保证仅针对规划模型，不适用于物理车辆。

Abstract: Optimisation-based parking planners usually impose collision constraints only at the time samples, so a vehicle corner can cut an obstacle between samples, and no executable trajectory exists until the solver converges. We present a planner for car-like vehicles with reverse gear in which every iterate of the optimisation phase satisfies the discretised dynamics exactly and keeps the whole vehicle rectangle clear of obstacles between the samples. Each time interval receives one convex corridor that holds all vehicle corners at both ends and is shrunk by a sweep margin bounding how far the corner paths leave their chords. Separating half-planes give the corridors a direction out of obstacles when the initial guess is in collision; later, heading-aligned boxes are grown from the speed, curvature and step of the current iterate and rebuilt after accepted steps. A feasibility-perturbed trust-region sequential quadratic programming method projects each step onto the dynamics by feedback and verifies it exactly; the cost decreases monotonically, and once the corridors stop changing, limit points are Karush-Kuhn-Tucker points of the corridor-constrained problem or violate a constraint qualification. On 820 benchmark cases the planner succeeds in 818 without penetration (797 from the first initial guess), none of the 7103 evaluated optimisation-phase iterates is unusable, and it succeeds in 96.5% of the cases when 99% of the initial guesses intersect an obstacle. Its maneuvers take 0.7% longer in the median than those of a similarly certified exact-collision baseline. The guarantees hold for the planning model, not for a physical vehicle.

</details>


### [92] [What the Guard Misses, the Robot Executes: Implied Harm in VLA Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05818)
*Sripad Karne,Arjun Balaji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现机器人视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）无法拒绝有害请求，且文本监控器对隐含有害意图的检测能力极弱，激活监控也无法弥补这一缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型会直接执行指令而无法拒绝，因此需要依赖监控器来筛查有害请求。作者想探究监控器能否识别"任务本身普通、但出于有害原因被请求"的情况，即在保持任务不变的前提下，仅改变意图表述的显性程度。

Method: 在保持任务固定的条件下，仅改变意图表述的显性程度，测试π_0.5模型及文本守卫的表现；同时测试基于模型激活值的监控方法（线性探针），在基础语言模型、视觉-语言模型以及机器人训练后的模型中区分有害与无害指令。

Result: π_0.5在所有显性程度上都能完成任务，频率与无害对照组相当。文本守卫能标记几乎所有直白请求，但对隐含请求几乎无效：高达95%的隐含有害运行任务完成且未被标记，即便针对机器人指令重新校准后仍高达90%。激活监控无法弥补这一缺口。线性探针在基础语言和视觉-语言模型中几乎能完美区分有害与无害指令，但经机器人训练后在两个模型家族中这种区分能力都会减弱，隐含有害情形减弱最明显。

Conclusion: 现有监控手段（文本守卫与激活监控）均难以捕捉隐含有害意图的机器人任务请求，且机器人训练会削弱模型内部对有害性表征的区分能力，凸显出VLA安全筛查的重大隐患。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) act on instructions without being able to refuse, so screening harmful requests falls to monitors. We test whether these monitors catch ordinary robot tasks requested for harmful reasons, holding the task fixed while varying only how explicitly the intent is stated. $π_{0.5}$ completes the task at every level of explicitness, as often as for harmless controls. Text guards flag nearly every blunt request but few implied ones: up to 95% of implied-harm runs end with the task done and no flag raised, and up to 90% even after recalibrating on robot instructions. Monitoring the model's activations does not close this gap. Linear probes separate harmful from harmless instructions almost perfectly in the base language and vision-language models, but this separation weakens after robot training in two model families, most for implied harm.

</details>


### [93] [Transporting Unsecured Stacked Payloads with a Quadrupedal Robot via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05819)
*Nobuo Namura,Masayuki Hiromoto,Kento Uemura,Hironobu Sasaki,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PAMORT方法，通过多目标强化学习与在线偏好自适应，实现四足机器人无固定、无传感器地稳定运输堆叠箱体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿足机器人在崎岖地形运输无固定载荷时，需在运动性能与载荷稳定性之间权衡：过于激进的动作即便机器人自身稳定，也可能使载荷失稳。现有方案多依赖专用载荷传感器或主动承载机构。

Method: PAMORT先训练一个以偏好向量为条件的多目标基础策略，该向量对运动奖励组与载荷稳定性奖励组进行加权；随后在冻结的基础策略上训练权重调节器，利用本体感知信息在线调整偏好。任务设定为无边缘躯干安装板上的无固定堆叠箱体运输。

Result: 仿真中，PAMORT在不同载荷配置下总体运输成功率与单目标基线相当或更优，并泛化到训练中未见的三箱堆叠。Unitree Go2真机实验中实现零样本迁移至不低于训练难度的斜坡与台阶，八个任务平均成功率0.850，基线为0.675。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅凭本体感知信息在线自适应运动—载荷权衡，即可实现稳健的无固定载荷运输。

Abstract: Transporting unsecured payloads with legged robots over uneven terrain requires balancing locomotion performance and payload stability, since aggressive motion can destabilize the payload even when the robot remains stable. We study quadrupedal transportation of unsecured stacked boxes on an edgeless torso-mounted board without dedicated payload sensors or active carrier mechanisms. To address this trade-off, we propose Payload-Adaptive Multi-Objective Reinforcement learning for Transportation (PAMORT). PAMORT trains a multi-objective base policy conditioned on a preference vector that weights locomotion and payload-stability reward groups, then trains a weight adjuster on the frozen policy to adapt this preference online from proprioception. In simulation, PAMORT achieves comparable or better overall transportation success than a corresponding single-objective baseline across different payload configurations, including an unseen three-box stack, despite training only with two boxes. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 demonstrate zero-shot transfer to slopes and steps at or beyond the training difficulty, with mean success rates of 0.850 for PAMORT and 0.675 for the baseline across eight tasks. These results demonstrate robust unsecured-payload transportation with online adaptation of the locomotion--payload trade-off from proprioceptive information.

</details>


### [94] [Dual-Rate Force-Image Control with Model-Based Orientation Limits for Robotic Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05839)
*Tyler Foster,Qiang Zhang,A B M Tahidul Haque,Anh Thu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人超声中力控制与图像反馈存在的双速率时延耦合问题，推导了闭式旋转速率上限，将其实现在双速率控制器中，并在明胶体模上验证其可有效降低初始接触力误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人超声中，高速接触力环与较慢且带延迟的图像反馈相互耦合，图像引导下的探头旋转会在图像响应被观测到之前扰动接触力。因此需要建立旋转速率与力偏移之间的显式约束，以在保证图像引导收敛的同时限制力扰动。

Method: 推导有限时域内旋转诱导估计力偏移的闭式方向速率上限，该上限考虑快力环的扰动抑制，并依赖局部接触刚度、力环增益、保守的旋转-力增益界、偏移预算与预测时域。将模型实现于双速率控制器中，采用基于时间戳的延迟重建与基于关节力矩的力估计。在曲面明胶体模上通过配对控制器比较与组件消融进行评估。

Result: 相较于无约束图像引导，所提出的速率受限控制器将第一秒估计力误差的RMS降低0.40 N，同时使线索收敛时间增加0.94 s。采用接近解析预测上限的固定速率上限时，力误差无显著差异，且收敛快0.32 s。延迟重建在测试延迟下无可分辨影响。单受试腘动脉扫描展示可行性，但图像线索在异质组织上受噪声限制。

Conclusion: 该模型的主要实用价值在于提供速率设计规则，而非在线预测；速率受限控制可有效降低初始力误差，但会以收敛速度略有下降为代价。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound couples a high-rate contact-force loop with slower, delayed image feedback, so image-guided ultrasound probe rotation can perturb contact force before the resulting image response is observed. We derive a closed-form orientation-rate limit that bounds the modeled rotation-induced estimated-force excursion over a finite horizon while accounting for disturbance rejection by the fast force loop. The limit depends on local contact stiffness, force-loop gains, a conservative rotation-to-force gain bound, the excursion budget, and the prediction horizon. We implement this model in a dual-rate controller with timestamp-based delay reconstruction and joint-torque-based force estimation, and evaluate it on a curved gelatin phantom using paired controller comparisons and component ablations. Relative to unconstrained image guidance, the proposed rate-limited controller reduced first-second root-mean-square (RMS) estimated-force error by 0.40 N while increasing cue-convergence time by 0.94 s. A fixed rate cap near the analytically predicted ceiling produced no resolvable difference in force error and converged 0.32 s faster, indicating that the principal practical value of the model is the rate-design rule rather than online prediction. Delay reconstruction had no resolvable effect at the tested latency. A single-subject popliteal scan demonstrated feasibility, although the image cue was noise-limited on heterogeneous tissue.

</details>


### [95] [Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Collision-Free Locomotion of an Underactuated Biped](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05855)
*Jagannath Prasad Sahoo,Saurabh Kumar,Surya Prakash S. K.,Samiran Datta,Abhay Dwivedi,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层强化学习(HRL)框架，使欠驱动双足机器人在不破坏平衡的前提下完成避障导航，静态/动态成功率分别达98.0%与88.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人偏离路径避障会破坏平衡，尤其在无髋/踝侧摆、每腿仅四个驱动关节的欠驱动平台上，路径跟随与平衡的耦合最为严重。

Method: 采用HRL：高层策略输入机器人位姿、36条射线距离、运动障碍状态与滚动时域局部目标，每10个控制步输出身体速度指令(v_x, v_y, ω_yaw)；低层速度条件策略通过PD关节目标跟踪指令。两层用SAC联合训练；将收敛的、任务无关的步态冻结，由经典规划器(A*、RRT*、APF)以相同指令接口驱动形成对比基线。

Result: 每方法在随机化PyBullet环境各100次试验中，所提方法静态达98.0%、动态达88.0%，规划器混合基线最高仅78.0%与68.0%；路径长度与A*参考相差4%以内；消融验证各观测通道与奖励项均有实质贡献。

Conclusion: 分层强化学习结合速度条件低层跟踪，可在欠驱动双足平台上实现高成功率避障导航，且冻结策略可作为通用指令接口，优于经典规划器混合方案。

Abstract: A bipedal robot cannot deviate from its path to avoid an obstacle without disturbing its balance, and this coupling is most severe on underactuated platforms such as the biped considered here, which has four actuated joints per leg and no hip or ankle roll. This paper presents a Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) framework in which a High-Level (HL) policy observes the robot pose, 36 raycast proximity measurements, moving-obstacle states, and a receding-horizon local goal, and outputs a body-velocity command $(v_x, v_y, ω_{yaw})$ every ten control steps, while a velocity-conditioned Low-Level (LL) policy tracks each command through PD-controlled joint targets. Both policies are trained jointly with Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). Because the converged gait is task-agnostic, it is frozen and driven by classical planners over the same command interface, yielding three controlled baselines: SAC+A*, SAC+RRT*, and SAC+APF. Across 100 evaluation trials per method in randomized PyBullet environments, the proposed method reaches the goal in 98.0% of static and 88.0% of dynamic trials, against at most 78.0% and 68.0% for the planner hybrids, with path lengths within 4% of the A* reference, and ablations confirm that each observation channel and reward term contributes materially to this performance.

</details>


### [96] [Mulligan: Performance-Guided Data Collection for Efficient On-Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05882)
*Lars Ankile,Perry Dong,Rohan Bhowmik,Aneesh Muppidi,David D. Yuan,Shuran Song,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Mulligan方法，将初始状态分布作为决策变量，从失败状态和未尝试状态开始采集监督式部署数据，并借助价值函数利用失败数据，从而在固定预算下最大化高精度操作任务的策略提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人从人类演示学习时，每增加一次演示带来的收益随策略改进而递减；改用监督式部署（操作员摆放物体并在策略失败时干预）可持续改进。但现有均匀采集会大量浪费在策略已能处理的状态上，尤其在高精度操作任务中，失败往往集中在少量初始状态，因此需要更高效地利用有限的监督回合预算。

Method: Mulligan将初始状态分布设为决策变量，每轮回合都从已观测到的失败状态和未尝试状态开始采集，而非均匀采样。为进一步提升数据效率，在交互式模仿学习基础上引入价值函数，用包括模仿学习会丢弃的失败数据在内的全部数据进行训练，并结合基于价值的动作选择（HiL-IDQL+Mulligan）。

Result: 在三个真实任务（2,550个留出盲测回合）和两个仿真任务上，Mulligan在相同采集预算下优于均匀初始状态采样；与基于价值的动作选择结合后，HiL-IDQL+Mulligan将真实任务最终成功率提升10-34个百分点。在有操作员干预的情况下，人机团队完成98%的采集回合，保证策略学习过程中采集持续有效。

Conclusion: 将初始状态分布作为主动选择对象，并充分利用失败数据中的价值信息，可显著提高监督式部署下机器人学习的样本效率和高精度任务的成功率。

Abstract: Learning from human demonstrations is a reliable way to teach robots new tasks, but the gains from each additional demonstration shrink as the policy improves. Continued improvement can instead come from supervised deployment, where an operator places the objects and intervenes when the policy fails. We ask how to maximize improvement from a fixed budget of supervised episodes on high-precision manipulation tasks with wide ranges of object placements. We observe that failures can concentrate in a small subset of initial states, so uniform collection spends much of the operator's time on states the policy already handles. Mulligan makes the initial-state distribution a decision, starting each round's episodes at observed failures and untried states. To further improve data efficiency, we augment interactive imitation learning with a value function trained on all data, including failures that imitation discards. Across three real-world tasks evaluated on 2,550 held-out, blinded episodes and two simulated tasks, Mulligan outperforms uniform initial-state sampling at matched collection budgets, and combined with value-based action selection, HiL-IDQL+Mulligan, improves final real-task success by 10-34 percentage points. With operator interventions, the human-robot team completes 98% of collection episodes, remaining productive while the policy learns. Videos, code, and data are available at https://mulligan.page/.

</details>


### [97] [From Social Reasoning to Embodied Interaction: An Agentic Framework for Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05964)
*Ziyu Cheng,Yuewen Guo,Zhirui Liu,Dong Zhang,Haotao Lu,Jingyi Yu,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARISE是一个统一框架，在Sophia人形机器人上结合智能体推理与交互式社交具身，实现何时沟通、沟通什么以及如何用机器人本征动作表达的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然人机面对面交互要求机器人理解演化中的社交情境、决定介入时机并通过协调的身体行为表达意图。现有方法难以形成闭环：基础模型智能体推理与记忆能力强但缺乏身体；虚拟社交智能体不受真实机器人物理约束；实体社交机器人则往往只部分解决社交推理或具身表达。

Method: ARISE整合多模态情境理解、长期记忆、反应式与主动式交互，以决定何时以及沟通什么；并将社交意图通过流式执行转化为与语音、机械面部表情协调的机器人本征手势，在Sophia人形机器人上实现统一框架。

Result: 在Sophia上的广泛评估显示：感知交互质量强，具身行为富有表现力且协调良好，流式执行带来显著延迟降低。

Conclusion: 结果强调，为实现与人形机器人的自然交互，需要联合推理沟通什么、何时介入以及如何物理表达社交意图。

Abstract: Natural face-to-face human--robot interaction requires a robot to understand an evolving social situation, decide when to engage, and express its intent through coordinated physical behavior. Yet existing approaches rarely close this loop: foundation-model agents provide increasingly capable multimodal reasoning and memory but remain largely disembodied, while expressive virtual agents do not face the physical constraints of real robots, and physical social robots typically address social reasoning and embodied expression only partially. We present ARISE, a unified framework that bridges Agentic Reasoning and Interactive Social Embodiment on the Sophia humanoid robot. ARISE integrates multimodal context understanding, long-term memory, and reactive and proactive interaction to determine when and what to communicate, and translates social intent into robot-native gestures coordinated with speech and mechanical facial expressions through streaming execution. Extensive evaluations on Sophia demonstrate strong perceived interaction quality, expressive and well-coordinated embodied behavior, and substantial latency reductions through streaming execution. These results highlight the importance of jointly reasoning about what to communicate, when to engage, and how to physically express social intent for natural interaction with humanoid robots. Project Page: https://robosocial.github.io/

</details>


### [98] [A State Based Dispatch Controller for Hospital Delivery Robots with Shared Human and Infrastructure Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05971)
*Krzysztof Siwek,Aleksandra Świetlicka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种位置感知调度模型，将机器人准入与运输支持、共享电梯、充电和清洁耦合，基于33,079条医院请求进行重放，比较人工调度、资源感知控制器与邻近-负载基准，并检验更简单的截止时间准入规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人配送研究可能高估运输能力，因为前往取货点的行程和人工支持常被排除在建模日程之外，导致准入测试通过但实际服务被延误。

Method: 构建位置感知调度模型，将机器人准入与运输支持者、共享电梯、充电和清洁资源耦合；重放33,079条观测到的医院请求，缺失内容、截止时间、人员配置和完成时间作为显式情景假设；在30次配对重复中对人工调度、资源感知控制器和邻近-负载基准做两套完整网格比较；并做探索性扩展，在同一运行模型下测试更简单的截止时间准入规则。

Result: 取货行程增加了共享电梯组的负荷。在一个高人员配置情景中，将假设电梯容量从两部提升到四部，可使仅人工调度的迟到率从55.11%降至3.18%，超过调度方法之间的差异。直接的策略比较和机器人覆盖率区分了准入选择性与系统服务。

Conclusion: 分析解释了机器人为何能通过截止时间准入测试，却因人工交接和共享设施而延误服务；其贡献是可复现地评估耦合调度工作流，清晰区分准入、完成和恢复可用性。结果为条件性比较，并非对医院收益或释放临床能力的估计。

Abstract: Robot delivery studies can overstate transport capacity when travel to pickups and human support fall outside the modeled schedule. We formulate a location aware dispatch model that couples robot admission to transporter support and shared elevators, charging, and cleaning. The model replays 33,079 observed hospital requests; missing contents, deadlines, staffing, and completion times remain explicit scenario assumptions. Two full grids compare human dispatch, a resource aware controller, and a proximity and workload benchmark in 30 paired replications per scenario. An exploratory extension tests a simpler deadline admission rule under the same operating model. Pickup travel increases demand on a pooled elevator bank. In one high staffing case, raising assumed elevator capacity from two to four reduces human only lateness from 55.11\% to 3.18\%, exceeding the dispatch differences. Direct policy comparisons and robot coverage distinguish admission selectivity from system service. The analysis explains why a robot can pass a deadline admission test yet delay service through staff handoffs and shared facilities. Its contribution is a reproducible evaluation of coupled dispatch workflows, with a clear separation between admission, completion, and return to availability. The results are conditional comparisons, not estimates of hospital benefit or released clinical capacity.

</details>


### [99] [How (and How Not) to Use Data Augmentation in VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05994)
*Bram Grooten,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了图像增强对VLA模型强化学习后训练的影响，发现只在critic模块上应用增强、保持actor输入干净，能显著提升分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在真实机器人任务中表现良好，但面对大幅视觉分布外偏移时泛化能力不足。虽然用强化学习进行后训练有助于提升鲁棒性，但仍有较大改进空间。作者希望探究图像增强在VLA后训练中的作用与最佳实践。

Method: 系统研究图像增强对VLA后训练的影响。核心做法是：在RL更新时仅对critic模块进行图像增强，而在rollout和更新过程中保持actor的输入为干净图像。同时考察了多种增强类型和强度，并针对π_{0.5}和GR00T N1.5两个模型进行实验验证。

Result: 对π_{0.5}和GR00T N1.5，所提方法在LIBERO-Plus上的分布外成功率分别提升7.8和10.0个百分点；而对actor进行增强会导致训练完全崩溃。此外给出了不同增强类型与强度的效果对比。

Conclusion: VLA后训练中，图像增强应仅作用于critic，保持actor输入干净是关键。该方法能有效提升VLA模型的分布外泛化能力，并提供了改善VLA后训练泛化性的实用建议。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models currently demonstrate strong performance in a wide range of real-world robotics tasks. However, they often still lack the generalization ability to handle large visual out-of-distribution shifts. Post-training of VLAs with reinforcement learning (RL) has been shown to benefit robustness, but significant room for improvement remains. In this work, we systematically study the effect of image augmentation on VLA post-training. We find that it is crucial to augment only the critic module during RL updates, while leaving the actor's input clean during both rollouts and updates. For $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.5 this raises out-of-distribution success on LIBERO-Plus by $7.8$ and $10.0$ points respectively, while augmenting the actor collapses training entirely. We investigate a range of augmentation types and strengths, and provide practical recommendations for improving generalization in VLA post-training.

</details>


### [100] [Infant simulator with an embodied caregiver: Generating infant-perspective touch and vision during social interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05997)
*Noemi Vaculinova,Matej Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MIMo的护理者扩展平台，将婴儿模型转为可重放二元互动并生成密集婴儿视角观测的可控平台，初步展示触觉分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期发展发生在护理者-婴儿二元互动中，婴儿的感觉运动流受身体接触和面对面互动塑造；但发展机器人模拟器通常孤立建模婴儿，限制了护理者中介经验的研究。

Method: 在MuJoCo中对多模态婴儿模型MIMo进行护理者扩展：构建与MIMo形态兼容的关节式护理者模型，参数化自人体测量数据并可选缩放；提供重放自然护理者-婴儿抱持与安抚互动的工作流；记录并可视化婴儿第一人称多模态经验；引入来源感知接触日志，区分自我、护理者和环境产生的接触，并支持聚合成与人工编码可比的触觉率统计。

Result: 以触觉为示例展示工具效果：来源感知接触日志能够区分自我、护理者与环境接触，并聚合成可比人工编码的触觉率统计；系统同时提供触觉与自我中心视觉的多模态记录。

Conclusion: 该平台可作为多模态二元数据集的生成器（触觉与自我中心视觉），用于建模社会互动的发展；触觉分析仅为示例，平台更广泛面向护理者中介的发展研究。

Abstract: Early development unfolds in caregiver-infant dyads, where infants' sensorimotor streams are shaped by physical contact and face-to-face interaction. Yet developmental robotics simulators commonly model infants in isolation, limiting the study of caregiver-mediated experience. We present a caregiver-enabled extension of the Multi-Modal Infant Model (MIMo) in MuJoCo that turns MIMo into a controllable platform for replaying dyadic interaction and generating dense infant-perspective observations. The system provides (i) an articulated caregiver model compatible with MIMo morphologies, parameterized from anthropometrics and optionally resized to a recorded caregiver; (ii) a workflow to replay naturalistic caregiver-infant holding and soothing interactions; and (iii) logging and visualizing the infant's first-person multimodal experience (we show touch and vision). We showcase the tool on touch by introducing origin-aware contact logging that disambiguates self-, caregiver-, and environment-generated contact and supports aggregation into touch-rate statistics comparable to manual coding. While we showcase tactile analysis, the platform is intended more broadly as a generator of multimodal dyadic datasets (touch and egocentric vision) for modeling the development of social interaction.

</details>


### [101] [P3: Persistent Particle Planning for Constrained Diffusion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06002)
*Hikmet Simsir,Mahyar Fardinfar,Ozgur S. Oguz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Persistent Particle Planning (P3)，一种面向扩散模型控制的序贯蒙特卡洛框架，通过在重规划步骤间维护加权候选计划种群，实现测试时约束下的可行性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型能提供表达力强的轨迹先验，但将其适配到测试时约束时，需要在连续控制决策中保持可行性与一致性，而从头采样重规划代价高且易导致路线切换。

Method: P3在每步控制中对候选轨迹进行平移与部分重新加噪，在最新观测下细化，并通过约束感知的加权与重采样选择后续方案，无需重训扩散模型；将去噪与重规划视为一个Feynman–Kac粒子系统，并在理想化修复假设下进行分析。

Result: 理论证明重新加噪深度决定保留计划保持原路线的可靠性，且保留稀有且分离良好的路线所需计划数远少于从头采样；实验中种群复用减少路线切换、提升成功率且无约束违反，每次重规划所需去噪迭代更少，在迷宫导航任务上比重新生成种群及约束优化单样本方法更快。

Conclusion: P3通过细化而非重绘先前计划，在测试时约束下兼顾效率与可行性，为扩散控制提供有效的序列化粒子规划方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide expressive priors over trajectories, but adapting these priors to test-time constraints requires maintaining feasibility and consistency across successive control decisions. We introduce Persistent Particle Planning (P3), a sequential Monte Carlo framework for diffusion control that maintains a weighted population of candidate plans across replanning steps. At each control step, P3 shifts and partially re-noises the candidate trajectories, refines them under the latest observation, and uses constraint-aware weighting and resampling to select among alternative continuations without retraining the diffusion model. We consider denoising and replanning as one Feynman--Kac particle system and analyze it under an idealized repair. We prove that the re-noising depth controls how reliably a kept plan stays on its route, and that keeping a rare, well-separated route takes far fewer plans than rediscovering it by sampling from scratch. Experiments under multiple test-time constraint configurations show that population reuse reduces route switching and improves success without constraint violations. Because P3 refines earlier plans instead of redrawing them, it also needs fewer denoising iterations per replan. On maze-navigation tasks, it plans faster than both regenerated populations and methods that correct a single sampled plan by constrained optimization. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/p3-username/p3-anon.

</details>


### [102] [Recon2Servo: Robotic Ultrasound Visual Servoing via Learned Image-to-Motion Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06010)
*Yameng Zhang,Pei Liu,Dianye Huang,Yizhao Qian,Zhongyu Chen,Xiangyu Chu,K. W. Samuel Au,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Recon2Servo框架，从B超图像直接学习图像到运动的6自由度探针控制，实现自主机器人超声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声视觉伺服对自主机器人超声至关重要，但仅凭2D B超图像实现6自由度探针控制困难，因为平面外运动线索有限且模糊。现有方法依赖解剖先验或手工视觉特征，跨成像目标的泛化性受限。

Method: 受无追踪器3D超声重建启发，采用DINOv3编码器结合低秩适配（LoRA）与双向关系模块，估计当前图像与目标图像间的相对探针位姿，引导迭代闭环目标视图对齐；融合监督式相对位姿学习、重建引导的闭环自适应以及有界残差位姿校正。

Result: 在公开数据集与自建数据集（12名健康志愿者、不同超声系统）上验证，展示了在重建体积伺服中的有效性；补充视频提供人体前臂目标视图对齐与动态跟踪的真机演示。

Conclusion: Recon2Servo实现了从B超图像直接学习6自由度探针控制，摆脱解剖先验和手工特征依赖，在体积伺服与真机目标对齐、动态跟踪中表现有效。

Abstract: Ultrasound visual servoing is essential for autonomous robotic ultrasound, yet 6-DoF probe control from 2D B-mode images remains challenging due to limited and ambiguous out-of-plane motion cues. Existing methods typically rely on anatomical priors or handcrafted visual features, limiting their generalizability across imaging targets. Inspired by trackerless 3D ultrasound reconstruction, we propose Recon2Servo, a visual servoing framework that learns image-to-motion inference directly from B-mode images for 6-DoF probe control. A DINOv3 encoder with low-rank adaptation and a bidirectional relation module estimate the relative probe pose between current and target images to guide iterative closed-loop target-view alignment. The framework combines supervised relative-pose learning, reconstruction-guided closed-loop adaptation, and bounded residual pose correction to improve motion inference during servoing. Evaluations on a public dataset and an in-house dataset collected from 12 healthy volunteers using different ultrasound systems demonstrate its effectiveness in reconstructed-volume servoing. Additional real-robot demonstrations of target-view alignment and dynamic tracking on a human forearm are provided in the supplementary video: https://youtu.be/qwsOdI-GMYk.

</details>


### [103] [MagServo: Uncertainty-Resilient Hierarchical Magnetic Servoing via Learned Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06046)
*Yuhan Tan,Yameng Zhang,Pei Liu,Yao Zhong,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MagServo，一种基于分层学习的6自由度磁伺服框架，直接利用学习到的潜在磁特征进行控制，无需解析磁模型或显式位姿估计，实现亚毫米、亚度级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁导航方法通常依赖显式位姿估计或直接使用原始磁测量，导致控制易受建模误差、测量噪声和扰动影响，精度与鲁棒性受限。

Method: 通过掩码重建学习抗不确定性的磁表征，并捕捉机器人运动与潜在磁变化之间的状态相关交互动力学；基于学习到的动力学，设计分层控制器，结合非线性模型预测控制（粗接近）与局部雅可比逆（精细调节）。

Result: 物理实验表明，6自由度位姿到达的平均终端误差为0.386 mm和0.479度；在复杂轨迹跟踪中优于基于定位的控制基线，并在未重新训练的未见磁源配置下保持鲁棒性能。

Conclusion: MagServo证明无需解析模型与显式位姿估计，仅凭学习到的潜在磁特征即可实现高精度、鲁棒的6自由度磁伺服控制。

Abstract: Magnetic navigation provides contact-free and line-of-sight-independent feedback for robotic systems, yet existing approaches typically rely on explicit pose estimation or direct use of raw magnetic measurements, making accurate control susceptible to modeling errors, measurement noise, and disturbances. This work presents MagServo, a hierarchical learning-based framework for robust 6-DoF magnetic servoing directly using the learned latent magnetic feature. MagServo learns uncertainty-resilient magnetic representations through masked reconstruction and captures state-dependent interaction dynamics between robot motion and latent magnetic transitions without analytical magnetic models or explicit Jacobian supervision. Based on the learned dynamics, a hierarchical controller combines nonlinear model predictive control for coarse approach with local Jacobian inversion for precise fine regulation. Extensive physical experiments demonstrate submillimeter and subdegree accuracy, achieving mean terminal errors of 0.386 mm and 0.479 degree for 6-DoF pose reaching. MagServo further outperforms a localization-based control baseline in complex trajectory tracking and maintains robust performance under unseen magnetic-source configurations without retraining. A supplementary video of the real-robot experiments is available at https://youtu.be/rZt1NUP1Mr0.

</details>


### [104] [Do VLAs Understand and Adapt to the Objects They Handle, or Simply Replay Learned Behaviors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06078)
*Xinnuo Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文探究VLA（视觉-语言-动作模型）的泛化究竟源于对物体物理属性的全局理解，还是对已学动作的重复回放，发现物理属性编码薄弱且未被可靠用于动作适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者想区分两种泛化：一种是真正基于对物体物理属性（如质量、易碎性）的理解来调整动作，另一种只是恰好在新场景中回放已学会的动作（偶然鲁棒性）。这一区分对评估VLA的真实泛化能力至关重要。

Method: 1）对七个VLA模型的激活进行线性探针和表征相似性分析（RSA），比较物理属性与非物理属性（语义类别、材料、声音、价格）的可解码性，并与基座VLM对比。2）在受控的LIBERO案例研究中，增加域内物体的质量，并通过语言或视觉信号提示该变化，观察策略动作是否相应调整。

Result: 几乎所有模态流中，物理属性（质量、易碎性、可变形性、摩擦、尺寸）的可解码性都低于非物理属性。相比基座VLM，机器人预训练削弱了语言流中物理属性的线性编码；预训练和下游微调都未增强物理属性差异与激活距离的对齐。在LIBERO案例中，多数VLA对更重物体与原始质量物体使用相似的抬起行为，导致任务成功率下降；少数例外者是根据词汇或视觉线索而非质量本身改变行为。

Conclusion: VLA对物理属性的编码较弱，且不能可靠地利用这些属性来调整动作。其泛化更多表现为对已学动作的回放所带来的偶然鲁棒性，而非真正基于物理理解的泛化。

Abstract: This paper asks whether VLA generalization is grounded in a global understanding of objects' physical properties that enables policies to adapt their motion to unseen setups, or if policies simply replay the motions they've learnt that happen to succeed in new setups. The former reflects genuine generalization; the latter reflects incidental robustness. We first examine awareness of physical properties in seven VLAs by applying linear probing and representational similarity analysis (RSA) to their activations. We find that physical properties, including mass, fragility, deformability, friction and size are less decodable than non-physical properties such as semantic category, material, sound and price in nearly every modality stream. Compared with their base VLMs, robot pre-training weakens the linear encoding of physical properties in the language stream. Neither pre-training nor downstream fine-tuning strengthens the alignment between physical-property differences and activation distances. We then ask whether the weak physical information present in these activations shapes the actions a VLA generates. In a controlled LIBERO case study, we increase the mass of an in-domain object and signal the change through language or vision. Most VLAs use similar lifting behaviour for the heavier and original-mass objects, leading to task success declines. The few exceptions change their behaviour in response to lexical or visual cues rather than to mass itself. These results suggest that VLAs encode physical properties weakly and do not reliably use them to adapt their motion.

</details>


### [105] [Adaptive Mean Flow for Responsive Closed-Loop Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06089)
*Aksel Vaaler,Marco Job,Christian Holden,Olav Egeland*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Adaptive Mean Flow (AMF)，一种基于流的模仿学习方法，通过加速的Mean Flow减少预测延迟，并利用上一步轨迹的加噪版本保证预测的平滑与一致，从而实现平滑、响应式的全闭环机器人控制，在仿真和真实任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流式机器人策略性能高、能建模连续多模态分布，但迭代去噪带来显著预测延迟，妨碍高频闭环控制并导致抖动。因此通常牺牲响应性、预测动作块顺序执行而不使用反馈。

Method: AMF采用Mean Flow（Flow Matching的加速形式）最小化预测延迟；在预测新动作时使用上一步轨迹的加噪版本，且信噪比随轨迹时间参数增大，从而抑制相邻步预测的剧烈变化，同时保留对未来步骤的自适应能力。

Result: 在大量仿真和真实机器人任务上评估AMF，与基线相比性能显著提升。

Conclusion: AMF实现了平滑、响应式的全闭环机器人控制，兼顾低延迟与预测一致性，验证了其在机器人模仿学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion- and flow-based robot policies have recently become widespread in robotic Imitation Learning (IL) due to their high performance and ability to model continuous and multimodal distributions. However, the iterative denoising procedure used by these models introduces significant prediction latency, hindering high-frequency closed-loop robot control and leading to jittery, unstable motion when frequent updates to the robot's action predictions are used. Therefore, it is common practice to train models to predict chunks of actions that can be executed sequentially without feedback, even when this reduces responsiveness and may mean the most recent state information is not used. In this article, we present Adaptive Mean Flow (AMF), a flow-based IL method that enables smooth and responsive, fully closed-loop robot control. AMF uses Mean Flow, which is an accelerated form of Flow Matching (FM), to minimize prediction latency. To ensure smoothness and consistency across predictions, AMF uses a corrupted version of the trajectory from the previous step when predicting new robot actions, with the signal-to-noise ratio increasing over the time parameter of the trajectory. This discourages large changes in the prediction from one step to the next, while allowing freedom to adapt the predictions for future steps. We evaluate AMF across a wide range of simulated and real robot tasks and demonstrate significantly improved performance compared with baselines. Code: https://github.com/akselva/Adaptive-mean-flow-RoboticIL.

</details>


### [106] [Execution-Aligned Progressive Noise for Consistent Asynchronous Replanning in Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06090)
*Di Wu,Ping Liu,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Lingfeng Zhang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出执行对齐渐进噪声(EAPN),在块间与块内引入结构化随机性,解决异步重规划中独立随机初始化导致的模式切换与延续不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时生成式机器人策略需要连续异步重规划,但独立随机初始化会造成动作块间模式切换、延续不连贯。

Method: EAPN在块间传播共享噪声轨迹并与实际执行位移对齐,建立执行对齐的块间相关性;块内建模动作时间的时序相关性;并将对齐的随机历史与已执行动作上下文结合以条件化后续生成。

Result: 在D3IL、Kinetix、LIBERO及真实操作任务上评估:D3IL多模态行为一致性提升,Kinetix平均成功率88.59%;LIBERO在长推理延迟下保持稳健;真机Object Storage成功率90.0%,双臂Cloth Folding成功率96.7%。

Conclusion: EAPN通过执行对齐的噪声结构化设计,实现了异步重规划下可靠、一致的连续生成式机器人执行。

Abstract: Continuous asynchronous replanning is essential for real-time generative robot policies, but independent stochastic initialization can cause mode switching and inconsistent continuation across action chunks. We propose Execution-Aligned Progressive Noise (EAPN), which introduces structured stochasticity at both inter-chunk and intra-chunk levels. Across replanning steps, EAPN propagates a shared noise trajectory and aligns it with the actual execution displacement, establishing execution-aligned inter-chunk correlation. Within each action chunk, it models temporal correlation along action time. The aligned stochastic history is further combined with committed action context to condition subsequent generation, allowing new chunks to continue from execution-consistent generative states rather than restart from independent noise. We evaluate EAPN on D3IL, Kinetix, LIBERO, and real-world manipulation tasks. EAPN improves multimodal behavior consistency on D3IL and achieves an average success rate of 88.59% on Kinetix. On LIBERO, it remains robust and maintains strong task performance even under long inference delays. Real-robot experiments further achieve 90.0% success on Object Storage and 96.7% on bimanual Cloth Folding, demonstrating reliable continuous execution under asynchronous replanning.

</details>


### [107] [Propagating Elevation-Map Uncertainty Through the Contact Maximum in Closed Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06103)
*Aleš Kučera,Karel Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出用Clark成对递归解析计算刚性接触最大值的矩，替代一阶线性化与蒙特卡洛采样，在保持微秒级速度的同时大幅降低风险感知路径规划的均值与CVaR误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险感知规划器依赖不确定高程图上的路径代价均值与标准差，但刚性接触需对多个不确定栅格取最大值。一阶传播只在单一栅格处求导而损失精度，蒙特卡洛则每抽样一次就要完整评估路径，成本高昂。

Method: 使用Clark成对递归在闭式下计算接触最大值的各阶矩；通过追踪每个接触与共享地图栅格的协方差，用精确高斯二次型恒等式传播平滑余项；再以在两条设计穿越上一次性拟合的"contest-depth"校准修正残余的标准差不足。

Result: 在317条留出路径段上、以同一信念的蒙特卡洛为参考，全部预注册标准均通过：矫正Clark折叠将均值中位误差从线性化的2.3%降至0.19%，在90%水平CVaR上取得所有方法中最低误差；单次基准规划在GPU上仅需5.5微秒；精度提升集中在争议接触处，选路虽少变但能以98%的比例选出参考最优路径（线性化93–96%）。第二个数据集上均值可迁移，风险数值不可迁移。

Conclusion: 矫正后的Clark递归为刚性接触提供了解析、精确且极快的风险传播，显著优于线性化，并在多数决策中选出参考最优路径；但风险数值的跨数据集泛化性有限，均值迁移性好而风险量不可直接迁移。

Abstract: Risk-aware planners score paths on uncertain elevation maps using the path cost's mean and standard deviation. Modeling rigid contact, however, requires computing a maximum over several uncertain cells. First-order propagation loses accuracy here by differentiating at only a single cell, while Monte Carlo sampling requires a full path evaluation per draw. We compute the moments of that contact maximum in closed form using Clark's pairwise recursion. By tracking each contact's covariance against the shared map cells, we propagate the smooth remainder using exact Gaussian quadratic-form identities. A contest-depth calibration, fitted once on two design traverses, closes the aggregate standard-deviation shortfall that remains. On 317 held-out rover path segments, scored against a Monte Carlo reference from the same belief, every pre-registered criterion was met. The corrected Clark fold cuts the median error of the mean from linearization's 2.3% to 0.19% and attains the lowest error in the conditional value at risk (CVaR) at the 90% level of every method tested. A benchmark plan costs just 5.5 microseconds on a GPU. These accuracy gains concentrate at contested contacts. While they seldom change which path is chosen on this terrain, the fold still selects the reference-best path in 98% of decisions against linearization's 93 to 96%. On a second dataset the mean transfers, though the risk number does not.

</details>


### [108] [Conditional Trajectory Peaks: Single-Pass Multimodal Policies over Action Chunks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06104)
*Di Wu,Rongtian Shen,Ping Liu,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Lingfeng Zhang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出CTP（Conditional Trajectory Peaks），一种单次前向传播的策略框架，同时预测完整的动作块候选、概率质量与轨迹尺度，实现多模态行为、跨块一致性与高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模仿学习需要满足两个关键需求：在相同观测下生成多样的可执行未来轨迹，以及在重规划周期之间保持行为一致性。现有方法难以同时兼顾多模态表达与闭环一致性，且推理效率存在瓶颈。

Method: 提出CTP框架，单次预测动作块候选、概率质量及轨迹尺度。包含两个组件：DAPS（分布感知峰值特化）利用轨迹级后验责任及质量与尺度调制的重叠约束来特化轨迹峰值；ETBT（证据门控轨迹信念传输）通过可交换候选集间的几何对应关系维持跨块一致性，同时允许当前策略证据覆盖历史约束。

Result: Push-T覆盖分数91.40%；D3IL的Avoiding、Aligning、Sorting-2成功率分别为100.0%、79.72%、84.44%；LIBERO平均成功率97.25%；真实双臂两盘任务中保留两种放置模式，50次试验全部成功；瓶子扶正与笔放入笔架任务成功率与π_0.5相当，但推理延迟从218.24 ms降至75.80 ms。

Conclusion: 结果表明单次轨迹建模能够同时结合多模态行为、闭环一致性与高效推理，验证了CTP框架的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal imitation learning requires diverse executable futures under the same observation and consistent behavior across replanning cycles. We present Conditional Trajectory Peaks (CTP), a single-pass policy framework that jointly predicts complete action-chunk candidates, probability masses, and trajectory scales. Distribution-Aware Peak Specialization (DAPS) specializes trajectory peaks using trajectory-level posterior responsibilities and mass- and scale-modulated overlap constraints. Evidence-Gated Trajectory Belief Transport (ETBT) maintains cross-chunk consistency through geometric correspondence between exchangeable candidate sets, while allowing current policy evidence to override historical constraints. CTP achieves a coverage score of 91.40% on Push-T; success rates of 100.0%, 79.72%, and 84.44% on D3IL Avoiding, Aligning, and Sorting-2, respectively. On LIBERO, CTP achieves an average success rate of 97.25%. In real-world dual-arm experiments, CTP preserves both placement modes in a two-plate task, succeeding in all 50 trials. On bottle uprighting and pen placement into a holder, it maintains success rates comparable to $π_{0.5}$ while reducing policy inference latency from 218.24 ms to 75.80 ms. These results demonstrate that single-pass trajectory modeling can combine multimodal behavior, closed-loop consistency, and efficient inference.

</details>


### [109] [Radar2Plan: Benchmarking 4D Radar for End-to-End Open-Loop Ego-Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06121)
*Ling Yao,Yichun Xiao,Jin Jin,Yihan Zhang,Fangqiang Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Radar2Plan，首个基于真实4D雷达数据的开源环自动驾驶轨迹规划基准，通过模块化接口统一比较传感器与规划器组合，验证4D雷达在恶劣天气和光照下可支持竞争性的轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气与不良光照是自动驾驶轨迹规划的主要挑战。4D雷达具备恶劣条件下的可靠感知能力和直接径向速度测量，但感知鲁棒性未必能转化为下游规划的鲁棒性，且现有4D雷达基准主要聚焦感知而非轨迹规划，缺乏统一评测平台。

Method: 提出Radar2Plan模块化基准，用于基于真实4D雷达数据的开环轨迹规划。通过通用接口连接传感器编码器、场景表示与规划头，实现不同传感器与规划器配置的受控比较。在DSERT-RoLL和MAN TruckScenes数据集上，采用统一协议，评估相机、4D雷达、LiDAR共七种组合，覆盖四种代表性规划基线和12种天气与光照条件。

Result: 实验表明，仅使用4D雷达即可支持具有竞争力的轨迹规划，并在恶劣条件下保持鲁棒性能。传感器配置对比显示4D雷达对其他模态具有互补价值，同时揭示了性能对传感器组合与规划架构的依赖关系。

Conclusion: Radar2Plan是首个专门评估真实4D雷达用于自动驾驶规划的基准，其模块化设计可扩展至更多数据集、感知模态与规划器，为雷达自动驾驶规划的未来研究提供灵活基础。

Abstract: Adverse weather and poor illumination remain major challenges for robust ego-trajectory planning in mobile autonomy. 4D radar offers reliable sensing under adverse conditions and direct radial-velocity measurements. However, sensing robustness does not necessarily translate into robust downstream planning, while existing 4D radar benchmarks focus primarily on perception rather than trajectory planning. We present Radar2Plan, a modular benchmark for open-loop ego-trajectory planning using real-world 4D radar data. Radar2Plan connects sensor encoders, scene representations, and planning heads through common interfaces, enabling controlled comparisons between different sensor and planner configurations. Using DSERT-RoLL and MAN TruckScenes, we evaluated seven combinations of camera, 4D radar, and LiDAR in four representative planning baselines and 12 weather and illumination conditions under a unified protocol. To our knowledge, Radar2Plan is the first benchmark dedicated to evaluating real-world 4D radars for autonomous driving planning. Experiments show that 4D radar alone supports competitive ego-trajectory planning and robust performance under adverse conditions. Sensor-configuration comparisons further demonstrate its complementary value to other modalities, while revealing dependencies on sensor combination and planning architecture. The modular design also supports additional datasets, sensing modalities, and planners, providing a flexible foundation for future research on radar-based autonomous driving planning.

</details>


### [110] [I-BFM: Reward-Conditioned Robust Humanoid Interaction via Unsupervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06129)
*Ziqi Han,Yitang Li,Junhan Sun,Fanrong Dong,Yaojie Shen,Lei Ye,Zetong Jing,Yongqi Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Xue Wang,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向人形-物体交互的行为基础模型 I-BFM，用前向-后向表示与无监督强化学习学习耦合动力学共享表示，单一策略可处理搬运、推、踢等多种任务并具备强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为基础模型虽能让人形机器人完成多样全身控制，但难以扩展到物理交互场景；传统方法依赖任务专用策略或参考轨迹跟踪，泛化性不足。

Method: 基于前向-后向表示与无监督强化学习，学习人形、物体及其接触之间耦合动力学的共享表示；给定下游任务奖励，同一策略可直接以潜在指令为条件执行闭环交互，无需任务专用策略优化；同时使用短时域交互目标与长时域目标联合训练。

Result: 单一 I-BFM 策略可完成搬运、推、踢，并支持目标到达、运动跟踪、风格控制与长时域任务链；在 Carry 任务上达到 94.3% 标称成功率，机器人跌倒后仍保持 89.3% 成功率，而规划基线仅 1.3%；Unitree G1 真机实验验证了多样移动操作、快速恢复与任务链能力。

Conclusion: I-BFM 表明通过共享耦合动力学表示可让人形机器人获得通用且鲁棒的物体交互能力，在严重偏差与外部扰动下仍有效，无需任务专用重训练。

Abstract: Behavioral foundation models (BFMs) have recently shown that a single humanoid policy can support diverse whole-body control, but extending such generality to physical interaction remains challenging. We introduce I-BFM, to our knowledge the first BFM for humanoid-object interaction. Rather than relying on task-specific policies or reference tracking, I-BFM learns a shared representation of the coupled dynamics among the humanoid, objects, and their contacts using forward-backward representations and unsupervised reinforcement learning. Given a downstream task reward, the same policy can be directly conditioned on a latent command to execute closed-loop interaction without task-specific policy optimization. To improve interaction control over different time scales, we further train the policy with both short-horizon interaction targets and longer-horizon goal targets. A single I-BFM policy performs carrying, pushing, and kicking, while also supporting goal reaching, motion tracking, stylistic control, and long-horizon task chaining. More importantly, it remains effective after large deviations from nominal execution: on Carry, I-BFM achieves 94.3% nominal success and retains 89.3% success after robot falls, compared with 1.3% for a planning-based baseline. Real-world experiments on a Unitree G1 further demonstrate diverse loco-manipulation behaviors, rapid recovery from interaction failures and external disturbances, and task chaining without task-specific retraining.

</details>


### [111] [Robotizing Human Videos with Physically Consistent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06137)
*Ching-Lam Cheng,Shengfeng He,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对人类视频用于机器人操作数据时的"具身差异"问题，从交互几何与场景可见性两个物理层面改进视频编辑流水线，实现更真实的手-机器人替换与遮挡合成，并通过与机器人演示联合训练提升策略成功率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频提供了可扩展的操作数据，但人手与机器人夹爪之间存在具身差异，无法直接使用。现有视频编辑方法（如Masquerade）用渲染的机器人替换人手，然而交互重建不准确与合成不当会导致抓取不一致、机器人与物体的遮挡不合理，从而限制了数据质量与下游策略学习效果。

Method: 提出两个互补模块：（1）交互感知接触重建模块，结合手-物分割与网格级接触预测恢复稠密3D接触，并将其转化为时序稳定的平行夹爪抓取；（2）深度感知合成模块，利用场景与机器人深度信息保证机器人与物体遮挡的物理一致性。生成的视频保留了人类演示的交互结构且与机器人兼容，并与机器人演示数据联合训练。

Result: 在使用相同人类视频与机器人数据的条件下，与纯机器人训练及原始Masquerade流水线相比，该方法在四个RoboTwin任务与两种Diffusion Policy视觉编码器上取得最高平均成功率，在分布外场景变化下提升尤为显著。真实世界部署显示，联合训练在任务几何可观测时能提升对视觉干扰物的鲁棒性，但深度敏感抓取任务受限于单相机配置，性能仍不理想。

Conclusion: 从交互几何与场景可见性两个物理角度修复视频编辑的失败模式，可有效将人类视频转化为机器人可用数据，联合训练显著提升策略性能与鲁棒性；未来需改进深度感知抓取能力（如多相机配置）以突破单相机限制。
